[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 10.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== gws gmail --help =====\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== Get-Content -Raw \'projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts\' =====\n// Verified Stripe + Cal.com commerce ingress. Every accepted response follows a\r\n// durable database commit; raw provider payloads and Booker PII never enter logs.\r\nimport { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\nimport Stripe from "npm:stripe@18.5.0";\r\nimport { grantStudentAccess } from "../_shared/grantStudentAccess.ts";\r\n\r\nimport { insertEvent, listEvents, meetUrlFromEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\r\nimport { hasConflictingCalendarEvent, SINGLE_SESSION, validateSingleSessionCheckout } from "../_shared/singleSessionCore.ts";\r\nimport { encryptEnvelope, generateCapabilityToken, hmacHex, sha256Hex } from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  listPaymentLinkLineItems,\r\n  retrievePaymentLink,\r\n} from "../_shared/stripe.ts";\r\n\r\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 256 * 1024;\r\nconst APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get("APP_ORIGIN") || "https://lsatprep.study";\r\n\r\nconst json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\r\n  status,\r\n  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },\r\n});\r\n\r\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\r\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\r\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction expectedLivemode(): boolean {\r\n  const value = requiredEnv("STRIPE_LIVEMODE").toLowerCase();\r\n  if (value !== "true" && value !== "false") throw new Error("stripe_livemode_invalid");\r\n  return value === "true";\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function readRaw(req: Request): Promise<string> {\r\n  const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());\r\n  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction safeEqual(a: string, b: string) {\r\n  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;\r\n  let difference = 0;\r\n  for (let index = 0; index < a.length; index++) difference |= a.charCodeAt(index) ^ b.charCodeAt(index);\r\n  return difference === 0;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function verifyCal(raw: string, signature: string | null) {\r\n  const secret = requiredEnv("CALCOM_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET");\r\n  return !!signature && safeEqual(await hmacHex(secret, raw), signature.replace(/^sha256=/i, ""));\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function matchStudent(supabase: any, email: string | null, name: string | null) {\r\n  if (email) {\r\n    const wanted = email.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n    let page = 1;\r\n    while (page <= 5) {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const users = data?.users ?? [];\r\n      const hit = users.find((user: any) => (user.email ?? "").trim().toLowerCase() === wanted);\r\n      if (hit) {\r\n        const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase\r\n          .from("profiles").select("class_id,display_name").eq("class_id", hit.id).maybeSingle();\r\n        if (profileError) throw profileError;\r\n        if (profile) return { id: profile.class_id, display_name: profile.display_name, email: hit.email };\r\n      }\r\n      if (users.length < 200) break;\r\n      page++;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  if (name) {\r\n    const normalized = name.trim().replace(/\\s+/g, " ");\r\n    const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n      .from("profiles").select("class_id,display_name").ilike("display_name", normalized).limit(2);\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    if ((data ?? []).length === 1) return { id: data[0].class_id, display_name: data[0].display_name, email };\r\n  }\r\n  return null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function calendarMirror(supabase: any, booking: { id: string; email: string | null; name: string | null; start: string; end: string }) {\r\n  const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n  const calendarId = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n  const event = await insertEvent(calendarId, {\r\n    summary: `${(booking.name || booking.email || "Student").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0]} session`,\r\n    description: "Booked through Cal.com and mirrored by LSAT U.",\r\n    start: { dateTime: booking.start },\r\n    end: { dateTime: booking.end },\r\n    attendees: booking.email ? [{ email: booking.email }] : undefined,\r\n    extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_calcom_booking_id: booking.id } },\r\n  }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: booking.email ? "all" : "none" });\r\n  const { data: mirrored, error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({\r\n    google_event_id: event.id,\r\n    calendar_id: calendarId,\r\n    title: event.summary,\r\n    description: event.description,\r\n    start_at: booking.start,\r\n    end_at: booking.end,\r\n    all_day: false,\r\n    status: event.status ?? "confirmed",\r\n    html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\r\n    meet_url: meetUrlFromEvent(event),\r\n    source: "calcom",\r\n    etag: event.etag ?? null,\r\n    last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n    deleted_at: null,\r\n  }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" }).select("id").maybeSingle();\r\n  if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n  return { googleEventId: event.id as string, scheduleEventId: mirrored?.id ?? null };\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function liveCalendarClear(supabase: any, booking: any): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (["booked", "refund_pending", "refunded", "refund_failed"].includes(booking.status)) return true;\r\n  const { data: state, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")\r\n    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  const ids = [...new Set([state?.calendar_id || "primary", ...(state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? [])])];\r\n  for (const calendarId of ids) {\r\n    const page = await listEvents(calendarId, {\r\n      timeMin: booking.start_at,\r\n      timeMax: booking.end_at,\r\n      singleEvents: "true",\r\n      showDeleted: "false",\r\n      maxResults: "50",\r\n    });\r\n    if (hasConflictingCalendarEvent(page?.items ?? [], booking.start_at, booking.end_at, booking.gcal_event_id)) return false;\r\n  }\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function expectedPaymentLinkFacts(stripeSecret: string, paymentLinkId: string) {\r\n  const link = await retrievePaymentLink(stripeSecret, paymentLinkId);\r\n  if (!link.active) throw new Error("inactive_payment_link");\r\n  const lineItems = await listPaymentLinkLineItems(stripeSecret, paymentLinkId);\r\n  if (!lineItems.length) throw new Error("payment_link_has_no_items");\r\n  let currency: string | null = null;\r\n  let amount = 0;\r\n  for (const item of lineItems) {\r\n    const price = item.price;\r\n    if (!price || price.unit_amount == null || !item.quantity) throw new Error("unsupported_payment_link_price");\r\n    currency ??= price.currency;\r\n    if (currency !== price.currency) throw new Error("mixed_payment_link_currency");\r\n    amount += price.unit_amount * item.quantity;\r\n  }\r\n  return { amount, currency };\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function handleStripe(supabase: any, raw: string, signature: string | null) {\r\n  const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n  if (!signature) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n  const stripe = new Stripe(stripeSecret, {\r\n    apiVersion: "2025-06-30.basil",\r\n    httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),\r\n  });\r\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\r\n  try {\r\n    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(\r\n      raw,\r\n      signature,\r\n      requiredEnv("STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET"),\r\n      300,\r\n    );\r\n  } catch (_) {\r\n    return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type.startsWith("refund.")) {\r\n    const refund = event.data.object;\r\n    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n    const bookingId = String(refund.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId || !refund.id || !refund.status) return json({ error: "invalid_refund" }, 400);\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("record_single_session_refund_status", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _stripe_refund_id: refund.id,\r\n      _provider_status: refund.status,\r\n    });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type === "checkout.session.expired") {\r\n    const session = event.data.object;\r\n    if (session.metadata?.product !== "single_session") return json({ received: true, ignored: "not_single_session" });\r\n    if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId || !session.id) return json({ error: "invalid_session" }, 400);\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("expire_single_session_checkout", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n      _reason: "stripe_checkout_expired",\r\n    });\r\n    if (error && error.code !== "P0409") throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (event.type !== "checkout.session.completed") return json({ received: true });\r\n  const session = event.data.object;\r\n\r\n  // Any completed payment opens the product for that email, whatever it was\r\n  // for. A single session, a package, a bootcamp: someone who has paid Joshua\r\n  // money is a student, and the branches below still do their own work.\r\n  if (session.payment_status === "paid") {\r\n    try {\r\n      const grant = await grantStudentAccess(\r\n        supabase,\r\n        session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,\r\n      );\r\n      console.log("access grant", { event: event.id, ...grant });\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      // Access is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to open\r\n      // it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was already recorded.\r\n      console.error("access grant failed", { event: event.id, err });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {\r\n    const validation = validateSingleSessionCheckout(session, expectedLivemode());\r\n    if (!validation.ok) return json({ error: validation.error }, 400);\r\n    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== "string") return json({ error: "missing_payment_facts" }, 400);\r\n    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n      .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id")\r\n      .eq("id", validation.bookingId).maybeSingle();\r\n    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;\r\n    if (!booking) return json({ error: "unknown_booking" }, 400);\r\n\r\n    const cancelToken = generateCapabilityToken();\r\n    const encryptedCancelPayload = await encryptEnvelope({\r\n      cancel_token: cancelToken,\r\n      cancel_url: `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/session?cancel_token=${encodeURIComponent(cancelToken)}`,\r\n    }, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"));\r\n    const { data: result, error } = await supabase.rpc("finalize_single_session_payment", {\r\n      _booking_id: validation.bookingId,\r\n      _provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n      _stripe_payment_intent_id: session.payment_intent,\r\n      _amount_cents: session.amount_total,\r\n      _currency: session.currency,\r\n      _livemode: session.livemode,\r\n      _live_calendar_clear: await liveCalendarClear(supabase, booking),\r\n      _cancel_token_hash: await sha256Hex(cancelToken),\r\n      _cancel_payload_ciphertext: encryptedCancelPayload,\r\n    });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Existing package branch, now protected by immutable payment validation.\r\n  if (session.mode !== "payment" || session.payment_status !== "paid") return json({ error: "payment_not_complete" }, 400);\r\n  if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n  const paymentLink = typeof session.payment_link === "string" ? session.payment_link : session.payment_link?.id;\r\n  if (!paymentLink) return json({ received: true, ignored: "not_booking_commerce" });\r\n  const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase.from("booking_offers")\r\n    .select("id,credits").eq("provider", "stripe").eq("provider_ref", paymentLink).eq("active", true).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (offerError) throw offerError;\r\n  if (!offer) return json({ received: true, ignored: "unknown_payment_link" });\r\n  const expected = await expectedPaymentLinkFacts(stripeSecret, paymentLink);\r\n  if (session.amount_total !== expected.amount) return json({ error: "amount_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n  if (String(session.currency || "").toLowerCase() !== expected.currency) return json({ error: "currency_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n  const email = session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null;\r\n  const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, null);\r\n  if (!student) {\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.from("booking_commerce_events").upsert({\r\n      provider: "stripe",\r\n      provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n      event_type: "unmatched_paid",\r\n      offer_id: offer.id,\r\n      email,\r\n      amount_cents: session.amount_total,\r\n      currency: session.currency,\r\n      payload: { checkout_session_id: session.id, payment_link: paymentLink, payment_status: session.payment_status, livemode: session.livemode },\r\n    }, { onConflict: "provider,provider_event_id,event_type" });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    return json({ received: true, match: "pending" });\r\n  }\r\n  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("apply_booking_package_payment", {\r\n    _student_id: student.id,\r\n    _offer_id: offer.id,\r\n    _provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n    _email: email,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return json({ received: true, student_id: student.id });\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n  try {\r\n    const raw = await readRaw(req);\r\n    const provider = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("provider");\r\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n    if (provider === "stripe") return await handleStripe(supabase, raw, req.headers.get("stripe-signature"));\r\n\r\n    if (provider === "calcom") {\r\n      if (!await verifyCal(raw, req.headers.get("x-cal-signature-256"))) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n      const event = JSON.parse(raw);\r\n      const payload = event.payload ?? event;\r\n      const eventType = String(payload.eventType?.slug ?? payload.type ?? "");\r\n      const bookingId = String(payload.uid ?? payload.bookingUid ?? payload.id ?? "");\r\n      const attendee = payload.attendees?.[0] ?? payload.responses ?? {};\r\n      const email = attendee.email ?? payload.email ?? null;\r\n      const name = attendee.name ?? payload.attendeeName ?? null;\r\n      const start = payload.startTime ?? payload.start_at ?? null;\r\n      const end = payload.endTime ?? payload.end_at ?? null;\r\n      if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "missing_booking_id" }, 400);\r\n      const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase.from("booking_offers")\r\n        .select("id,url").eq("provider", "calcom").eq("provider_ref", eventType).maybeSingle();\r\n      if (offerError) throw offerError;\r\n      const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, name);\r\n      const status = event.triggerEvent === "BOOKING_CANCELLED" ? "cancelled" : "booked";\r\n      let mirror: { googleEventId: string; scheduleEventId: string | null } | null = null;\r\n      if (student && status === "booked" && start && end) mirror = await calendarMirror(supabase, { id: bookingId, email, name, start, end });\r\n      const { error: externalError } = await supabase.from("external_booking_events").upsert({\r\n        provider_booking_id: bookingId,\r\n        student_id: student?.id ?? null,\r\n        offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\r\n        email,\r\n        attendee_name: name,\r\n        start_at: start,\r\n        end_at: end,\r\n        status,\r\n        source_url: offer?.url ?? null,\r\n        google_event_id: mirror?.googleEventId ?? null,\r\n        schedule_event_id: mirror?.scheduleEventId ?? null,\r\n        payload: { booking_id: bookingId, event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? null, start_at: start, end_at: end },\r\n      }, { onConflict: "provider_booking_id" });\r\n      if (externalError) throw externalError;\r\n      const { error: commerceError } = await supabase.from("booking_commerce_events").upsert({\r\n        provider: "calcom",\r\n        provider_event_id: event.id ?? `${bookingId}:${event.triggerEvent}`,\r\n        event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? "booking",\r\n        student_id: student?.id ?? null,\r\n        offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\r\n        email,\r\n        payload: { booking_id: bookingId, event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? null, start_at: start, end_at: end },\r\n      }, { onConflict: "provider,provider_event_id,event_type" });\r\n      if (commerceError) throw commerceError;\r\n      return json({ received: true, match: student ? "matched" : "pending" });\r\n    }\r\n    return json({ error: "unknown_provider" }, 400);\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "payload_too_large") return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\r\n    console.error("booking-commerce-webhook", { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n    return json({ error: "provider_processing_failed" }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== Get-Content -Raw \'projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/onboarding-intake-notify/index.ts\' =====\nimport { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "../_shared/sendAppEmail.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  buildOnboardingMarkdown,\r\n  type OnboardingProfile,\r\n} from "../_shared/onboardingMarkdown.ts";\r\n\r\n// Emails Joshua one Markdown summary when a student finishes the intake.\r\n//\r\n// Two ways in, one outcome:\r\n//   - the student\'s own browser calls this right after the completing save;\r\n//   - an admin calls it with { action: "sweep" } to catch anyone whose tab\r\n//     closed before that call landed.\r\n// Both stamp profiles.onboarding_notified_at, so nobody is ever mailed twice.\r\n\r\nconst corsHeaders = {\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst PROFILE_COLUMNS =\r\n  "class_id, display_name, onboarding, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at";\r\n\r\nfunction jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction adminClient() {\r\n  return createClient(\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!,\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function callerId(req: Request): Promise<string> {\r\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\r\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");\r\n  const anon = createClient(\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!,\r\n  );\r\n  const { data, error } = await anon.auth.getClaims(authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length));\r\n  if (error || !data?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");\r\n  return data.claims.sub as string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Sends for one profile and stamps it. Returns false when nothing was owed. */\r\nasync function notify(\r\n  admin: ReturnType<typeof adminClient>,\r\n  row: Record<string, unknown>,\r\n): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (!row.onboarding_completed_at) return false;\r\n  if (row.onboarding_notified_at) return false;\r\n\r\n  const classId = row.class_id as string;\r\n  const { data: authUser } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(classId);\r\n  const profile: OnboardingProfile = {\r\n    class_id: classId,\r\n    display_name: (row.display_name as string) ?? null,\r\n    email: authUser?.user?.email ?? null,\r\n    onboarding: (row.onboarding as Record<string, unknown>) ?? null,\r\n    onboarding_completed_at: row.onboarding_completed_at as string,\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const markdown = buildOnboardingMarkdown(profile);\r\n  await sendAppEmail("onboarding-intake", "", {\r\n    // Same key for the same student forever, so a retry cannot double-send.\r\n    idempotencyKey: `onboarding-intake:${classId}`,\r\n    replyTo: profile.email ?? undefined,\r\n    templateData: {\r\n      studentName: profile.display_name || profile.email || "Unknown student",\r\n      studentEmail: profile.email ?? "",\r\n      markdown,\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Stamped only after a send that did not throw. A failed send leaves the\r\n  // profile owed, and the next sweep retries it.\r\n  const { error } = await admin\r\n    .from("profiles")\r\n    .update({ onboarding_notified_at: new Date().toISOString() })\r\n    .eq("class_id", classId);\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\r\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const admin = adminClient();\r\n    const body = req.method === "POST" ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};\r\n\r\n    if (body?.action === "sweep") {\r\n      const uid = await callerId(req);\r\n      const { data: roleRow } = await admin\r\n        .from("user_roles")\r\n        .select("role")\r\n        .eq("user_id", uid)\r\n        .eq("role", "admin")\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (!roleRow) return jsonRes({ error: "Forbidden" }, 403);\r\n\r\n      const { data: owed, error } = await admin\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .select(PROFILE_COLUMNS)\r\n        .not("onboarding_completed_at", "is", null)\r\n        .is("onboarding_notified_at", null);\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      let sent = 0;\r\n      const failed: string[] = [];\r\n      for (const row of owed ?? []) {\r\n        // One student\'s bad address must not strand the rest of the queue.\r\n        try {\r\n          if (await notify(admin, row as Record<string, unknown>)) sent += 1;\r\n        } catch (err) {\r\n          console.error("sweep: send failed", { class_id: row.class_id, err });\r\n          failed.push(row.class_id as string);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n      return jsonRes({ success: true, sent, failed });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Default: the student who just finished, notifying about themselves.\r\n    const uid = await callerId(req);\r\n    const { data: row, error } = await admin\r\n      .from("profiles")\r\n      .select(PROFILE_COLUMNS)\r\n      .eq("class_id", uid)\r\n      .maybeSingle();\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    if (!row) return jsonRes({ error: "No profile" }, 404);\r\n\r\n    const sent = await notify(admin, row as Record<string, unknown>);\r\n    return jsonRes({ success: true, sent });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n    console.error("onboarding-intake-notify failed", message);\r\n    const status = message === "Unauthorized" || message === "Missing auth" ? 401 : 500;\r\n    return jsonRes({ error: message }, status);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== Get-Content -Raw \'projects/lsatu/src/lib/onboarding.ts\' =====\n// Shared onboarding constants + types. See ONBOARDING-SPEC.md.\r\n//\r\n// The onboarding intake is a one-time flow (gate: profiles.onboarding_completed_at)\r\n// whose answers persist to profiles.onboarding (jsonb). Two steps share one\r\n// score-band enum; experience and schools are optional; study time is defaulted.\r\n//\r\n// The intake runs inside the classroom\'s orientation card (OrientationCard),\r\n// never as a redirect: a new signup lands in the classroom and has to open the\r\n// card and click through it. Each Continue writes that step\'s answer, so a\r\n// half-finished card resumes where it left off.\r\n\r\n// ── Score bands (shared by "last LSAT" and "target" steps) ──────────────────\r\nexport const SCORE_BANDS = [\r\n  { value: \'lte_150\', label: \'150 & below\' },\r\n  { value: \'151_155\', label: \'151–155\' },\r\n  { value: \'156_160\', label: \'156–160\' },\r\n  { value: \'161_165\', label: \'161–165\' },\r\n  { value: \'166_170\', label: \'166–170\' },\r\n  { value: \'171_175\', label: \'171–175\' },\r\n  { value: \'176_180\', label: \'176–180\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type ScoreBand = (typeof SCORE_BANDS)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\n// Per-field extras appended after the shared bands.\r\n// "Haven\'t taken it" is no longer an option here: the taken_lsat step asks that\r\n// first and skips this step entirely, so the question is never put to someone it\r\n// does not apply to. The stored value survives for students who answered it\r\n// under the old flow.\r\nexport const LAST_SCORE_EXTRAS = [\r\n  { value: \'not_sure\', label: "I\'m not sure" },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport const TARGET_SCORE_EXTRAS = [\r\n  { value: \'not_sure\', label: "I\'m not sure yet" },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type LastScoreValue = ScoreBand | \'not_taken\' | \'not_sure\';\r\nexport type TargetScoreValue = ScoreBand | \'not_sure\';\r\n\r\n// ── Life stage (step 5) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\nexport const LIFE_STAGES = [\r\n  { value: \'undergraduate\', title: \'Undergraduate\', desc: "Currently pursuing a bachelor\'s degree" },\r\n  { value: \'graduate\', title: \'Graduate\', desc: \'Pursuing or completed a graduate degree\' },\r\n  { value: \'working\', title: \'Working\', desc: \'Currently employed full or part-time\' },\r\n  { value: \'other\', title: \'All Other Cases\', desc: \'None of the above apply to me\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type LifeStage = (typeof LIFE_STAGES)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\n// ── Study time per day (step 6) ─────────────────────────────────────────────\r\nexport const STUDY_TIME_OPTIONS = [\r\n  { value: \'none\', label: \'No time\' },\r\n  { value: \'lt_1\', label: \'< 1 hr\' },\r\n  { value: \'1_2\', label: \'1–2 hrs\' },\r\n  { value: \'3_plus\', label: \'3+ hrs\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type StudyTimeValue = (typeof STUDY_TIME_OPTIONS)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\nexport const DAYS = [\r\n  { key: \'sun\', label: \'Sun\' },\r\n  { key: \'mon\', label: \'Mon\' },\r\n  { key: \'tue\', label: \'Tue\' },\r\n  { key: \'wed\', label: \'Wed\' },\r\n  { key: \'thu\', label: \'Thu\' },\r\n  { key: \'fri\', label: \'Fri\' },\r\n  { key: \'sat\', label: \'Sat\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type DayKey = (typeof DAYS)[number][\'key\'];\r\n\r\nexport const DEFAULT_STUDY_TIME: Record<DayKey, StudyTimeValue> = {\r\n  sun: \'none\', mon: \'none\', tue: \'none\', wed: \'none\', thu: \'none\', fri: \'none\', sat: \'none\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n// ── Test date (asked right after the target score) ──────────────────────────\r\n// Stored twice on purpose: the ISO date lands in the real `profiles.test_date`\r\n// column (the classroom dateline counts down from it), and a copy lives in the\r\n// answers blob so "have they answered this step?" stays a single read. A\r\n// student who has not registered yet picks UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE, which answers\r\n// the step while leaving the column null — no invented date.\r\nexport const UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE = \'undecided\';\r\n\r\nexport type TestDateValue = string; // \'YYYY-MM-DD\' | typeof UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE\r\n\r\n/** The value written to profiles.test_date: a real date, or null when undecided. */\r\nexport function testDateColumnValue(value: TestDateValue | undefined): string | null {\r\n  if (!value || value === UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE) return null;\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ── The stored answer shape (profiles.onboarding) ───────────────────────────\r\nexport interface OnboardingAnswers {\r\n  has_taken_lsat?: boolean;\r\n  last_score_band?: LastScoreValue;\r\n  experience_text?: string;\r\n  schools?: string[];\r\n  target_score_band?: TargetScoreValue;\r\n  test_date?: TestDateValue;\r\n  life_stage?: LifeStage;\r\n  study_time?: Record<DayKey, StudyTimeValue>;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// The intake steps (name is step 0, handled before these). Order is locked to\r\n// the approved reference, with the test date slotted in after the target score\r\n// so the three "where are you headed" questions sit together.\r\nexport type OnboardingStepId =\r\n  | \'taken_lsat\'\r\n  | \'last_score\'\r\n  | \'experience\'\r\n  | \'schools\'\r\n  | \'target_score\'\r\n  | \'test_date\'\r\n  | \'life_stage\'\r\n  | \'study_time\';\r\n\r\n// Which answer field each step fills. Used to compute the first unanswered step\r\n// on resume. Steps whose field is optional never block resume-completion.\r\nexport const STEP_ORDER: OnboardingStepId[] = [\r\n  \'taken_lsat\',\r\n  \'last_score\',\r\n  \'experience\',\r\n  \'schools\',\r\n  \'target_score\',\r\n  \'test_date\',\r\n  \'life_stage\',\r\n  \'study_time\',\r\n];\r\n\r\n// Required steps gate the "first unanswered step" on resume. Optional steps\r\n// (experience, schools) are skippable within the flow without blocking.\r\nexport const REQUIRED_STEPS: Record<OnboardingStepId, boolean> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: true,\r\n  last_score: true,\r\n  experience: false,\r\n  schools: false,\r\n  target_score: true,\r\n  test_date: true, // answerable as "no date yet", so never a dead end\r\n  life_stage: true,\r\n  study_time: true, // defaulted, so effectively always satisfiable\r\n};\r\n\r\n// ── Copy ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n// One home for every headline the intake shows, so the card and any future\r\n// surface cannot drift into two versions of the same question.\r\nexport const NAME_STEP_COPY = {\r\n  title: \'What should we call you?\',\r\n  subtitle: \'This is the name your classroom and Joshua will use.\',\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const STEP_COPY: Record<OnboardingStepId, { title: string; subtitle?: string }> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: {\r\n    title: \'Have you taken the LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'An official, scored test. Practice tests do not count here.\',\r\n  },\r\n  last_score: {\r\n    title: \'What did you get on your last LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'This sets your starting point.\',\r\n  },\r\n  experience: {\r\n    title: \'How has your LSAT experience been so far?\',\r\n    subtitle:\r\n      \'Share what has felt easy and what has felt hard so we can personalize your plan.\',\r\n  },\r\n  schools: {\r\n    title: \'What schools are you interested in?\',\r\n    subtitle: "We\'ll build your experience with LSAT U around getting into these schools.",\r\n  },\r\n  target_score: {\r\n    title: \'What score are you aiming for?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'This helps us set the right pace for your prep.\',\r\n  },\r\n  test_date: {\r\n    title: \'When are you taking the LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'Your classroom counts down from this and paces your week around it.\',\r\n  },\r\n  life_stage: {\r\n    title: \'Which best describes you?\',\r\n  },\r\n  study_time: {\r\n    title: \'Build your study week\',\r\n    subtitle:\r\n      \'Show Joshua when LSAT prep can realistically fit. Start with the days you have, then choose the time you can protect on each one.\',\r\n  },\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Short row labels for the Account page, where the answers are listed rather\r\n// than asked. The full questions live in STEP_COPY and stay the card\'s voice.\r\nexport const STEP_LABELS: Record<OnboardingStepId, string> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: \'Taken the LSAT\',\r\n  last_score: \'Last LSAT score\',\r\n  experience: \'How it has gone so far\',\r\n  schools: \'Target schools\',\r\n  target_score: \'Target score\',\r\n  test_date: \'Test date\',\r\n  life_stage: \'Where you are at\',\r\n  study_time: \'Weekly study time\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The steps this student is actually asked, in order. Answering "no" to\r\n * taken_lsat drops the score step rather than showing it with an escape hatch,\r\n * which also keeps the "Step N of M" counter honest.\r\n */\r\nexport function visibleSteps(a: OnboardingAnswers): OnboardingStepId[] {\r\n  return STEP_ORDER.filter((step) => step !== \'last_score\' || a.has_taken_lsat !== false);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Whether a given step\'s answer is present. Drives resume and the Continue gate. */\r\nexport function isStepAnswered(step: OnboardingStepId, a: OnboardingAnswers): boolean {\r\n  switch (step) {\r\n    case \'taken_lsat\':\r\n      return typeof a.has_taken_lsat === \'boolean\';\r\n    case \'last_score\':\r\n      return !!a.last_score_band;\r\n    case \'experience\':\r\n      return !!a.experience_text?.trim();\r\n    case \'schools\':\r\n      return !!a.schools && a.schools.length > 0;\r\n    case \'target_score\':\r\n      return !!a.target_score_band;\r\n    case \'test_date\':\r\n      return !!a.test_date;\r\n    case \'life_stage\':\r\n      return !!a.life_stage;\r\n    case \'study_time\':\r\n      return !!a.study_time;\r\n    default:\r\n      return false;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829000000_onboarding_intake_notification.sql' =====\n-- One email per finished intake, ever.\r\n--\r\n-- The send is stamped on the profile rather than inferred from the mail log, so\r\n-- a student who finishes onboarding is notified exactly once even if the tab is\r\n-- closed mid-send and the nightly sweep picks them up later.\r\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS onboarding_notified_at timestamptz;\r\n\r\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.onboarding_notified_at IS\r\n  'When the onboarding intake email was sent to Joshua. Null means it is still owed.';\r\n\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829030000_finalize_free_booking.sql' =====\nGet-Content: \r\nLine |\r\n   2 |  Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829030000_f …\r\n     |  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\r\n     | Cannot find path 'C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\migrations\\20260829030000_finalize_free_booking.sql' because it does not exist.\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829020000_classroom_access_is_a_paid_flag.sql' =====\nGet-Content: \r\nLine |\r\n   2 |  Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829020000_c …\r\n     |  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\r\n     | Cannot find path 'C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\supabase\\migrations\\20260829020000_classroom_access_is_a_paid_flag.sql' because it does not exist.\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== Get-Content -Raw 'projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260630010000_classroom_access_auto_on.sql' =====\n-- Classroom access is no longer a per-student permission managed from the\r\n-- People page. Every student needs to reach /classroom to see the bootcamps\r\n-- assigned to them; bootcamp visibility itself is controlled by classroom_items\r\n-- assignments and enforced by BootcampGate, not by a global flag.\r\n--\r\n-- The column default is already true (see 20260416015637), so new students are\r\n-- covered. This backfills any existing student whose classroom access was\r\n-- revoked under the old broad-permission model.\r\nUPDATE public.profiles\r\nSET has_classroom_access = true\r\nWHERE has_classroom_access = false;\r\n\r\n"}]

Every existing student keeps\r\n-- the access they already have.\r\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\r\n  ALTER COLUMN has_classroom_access SET DEFAULT false;\r\n\r\n\n\n===== projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260830030000_finalize_free_booking.sql =====\n-- Booking something that costs nothing.\r\n--\r\n-- Every path to a confirmed booking runs through the Stripe webhook today, so a\r\n-- free event has no way to finish: it holds the slot, puts a tentative event on\r\n-- the calendar, and waits forever for a payment that will never arrive. This is\r\n-- the missing door -- the same promotion finalize_single_session_payment does,\r\n-- with the payment facts removed and nothing else changed.\r\n--\r\n-- The one rule that matters: this refuses outright on any event whose type says\r\n-- requires_payment. A free confirmation is not a shortcut that a paid session\r\n-- can be talked into taking, whatever the caller sends.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(\r\n  _booking_id uuid,\r\n  _expected_version integer,\r\n  _live_calendar_clear boolean,\r\n  _cancel_token_hash text,\r\n  _cancel_payload_ciphertext text\r\n) RETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $fn$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _requires_payment boolean;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'booking_not_found\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0404\'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  SELECT requires_payment INTO _requires_payment\r\n  FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\r\n  IF _requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_required\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  -- Already finished. Say so rather than doing it twice: the browser retries\r\n  -- this call, and a retry must not enqueue a second confirmation email.\r\n  IF _booking.status = \'booked\' THEN\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n      \'lifecycle_version\', _booking.lifecycle_version, \'was_existing\', true);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status <> \'draft\' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'stale_booking\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, \'\') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, \'\') IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'cancel_capability_required\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  -- The same last look the paid path takes: the calendar was clear a moment ago\r\n  -- and nothing else in this database holds the time.\r\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\r\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\r\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.id <> _booking.id AND b.status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\', \'booked\')\r\n        AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, \'[)\') && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, \'[)\')\r\n    )\r\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\r\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r\r\n      WHERE r.status = \'booked\'\r\n        AND tstzrange(r.start_at - interval \'15 minutes\', r.end_at + interval \'15 minutes\', \'[)\')\r\n            && tstzrange(_booking.start_at, _booking.end_at, \'[)\')\r\n    )\r\n    AND NOT EXISTS (\r\n      SELECT 1 FROM public.schedule_events se\r\n      WHERE se.deleted_at IS NULL AND se.status <> \'cancelled\'\r\n        AND (_booking.gcal_event_id IS NULL OR se.google_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking.gcal_event_id)\r\n        AND se.start_at < _booking.end_at AND se.end_at > _booking.start_at\r\n    );\r\n\r\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\r\n    -- Nothing was charged, so there is nothing to refund: the hold simply dies\r\n    -- and the tentative calendar event is swept.\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = \'expired\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      terminal_reason = \'slot_taken_before_confirm\'\r\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'gcal_delete\', \'system\', \'pending\',\r\n        \'expired\', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n    END IF;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n      \'lifecycle_version\', _booking.lifecycle_version, \'was_existing\', false);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n    status = \'booked\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = \'usd\',\r\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\r\n    terminal_reason = NULL\r\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'gcal_promote\', \'system\', \'pending\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, NULL);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_booking_confirmation\', \'booker\', \'blocked\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_admin_notification\', \'admin\', \'blocked\',\r\n    \'booked\', _new_version, NULL);\r\n\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'booking_id\', _booking.id, \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n    \'lifecycle_version\', _new_version, \'was_existing\', false);\r\nEND;\r\n$fn$;\r\n\r\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(uuid, integer, boolean, text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\r\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(uuid, integer, boolean, text, text) TO service_role;\r\n\r\n\n\n===== projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829020000_grant_onboarding_notified_update.sql =====\n-- "Start my setup again" clears three columns: onboarding, onboarding_completed_at\r\n-- and onboarding_notified_at. The first two were granted when the intake shipped;\r\n-- the notified stamp was added later and never was, so every student reset failed\r\n-- with a column-permission error ("Could not reset your setup").\r\n--\r\n-- Clearing the stamp is what makes the finished re-intake mail Joshua the new\r\n-- answers instead of being swallowed as a duplicate. RLS still confines the write\r\n-- to the student\'s own row.\r\nGRANT UPDATE (onboarding_notified_at) ON public.profiles TO authenticated;\r\n\r\n\n\n===== projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260718000000_profiles_onboarding.sql =====\n-- New-signup onboarding intake. Stores the placement answers on the profile\r\n-- (score/target bands, schools, free-text experience, life stage, weekly study\r\n-- time) plus a one-time completion gate. See ONBOARDING-SPEC.md.\r\n--\r\n-- onboarding: single jsonb blob, matches the existing layout_prefs/by_qtype_json\r\n--   pattern. Shape:\r\n--     {\r\n--       last_score_band, experience_text, schools: [],\r\n--       target_score_band, life_stage, study_time: { sun..sat }\r\n--     }\r\n-- onboarding_completed_at: the gate. Set ONCE (explicit Skip or Finish); a null\r\n--   value routes the user to /onboarding. Closing the tab never sets it.\r\nalter table public.profiles\r\n  add column if not exists onboarding jsonb,\r\n  add column if not exists onboarding_completed_at timestamptz;\r\n\r\n-- Backfill: every existing student counts as already-onboarded so only fresh\r\n-- signups ever see the flow. Runs in the same migration that adds the column so\r\n-- no active student is briefly gated into onboarding.\r\nupdate public.profiles\r\n  set onboarding_completed_at = now()\r\n  where onboarding_completed_at is null;\r\n\r\n\n\n===== projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260829010000_backfill_onboarding_notified.sql =====\n-- Everyone who onboarded before the notification existed is marked as already\r\n-- notified, so the sweep starts from an empty queue.\r\n--\r\n-- Without this, the first sweep would mail Joshua one message for every student\r\n-- who has ever finished the intake — thirty-odd emails about students he\r\n-- onboarded months ago. The notification is for new intakes only.\r\nUPDATE public.profiles\r\nSET onboarding_notified_at = now()\r\nWHERE onboarding_completed_at IS NOT NULL\r\n  AND onboarding_notified_at IS NULL;\r\n\r\n\n\n===== projects/lsatu/src/lib/onboarding.ts =====\n// Shared onboarding constants + types. See ONBOARDING-SPEC.md.\r\n//\r\n// The onboarding intake is a one-time flow (gate: profiles.onboarding_completed_at)\r\n// whose answers persist to profiles.onboarding (jsonb). Two steps share one\r\n// score-band enum; experience and schools are optional; study time is defaulted.\r\n//\r\n// The intake runs inside the classroom\'s orientation card (OrientationCard),\r\n// never as a redirect: a new signup lands in the classroom and has to open the\r\n// card and click through it. Each Continue writes that step\'s answer, so a\r\n// half-finished card resumes where it left off.\r\n\r\n// ── Score bands (shared by "last LSAT" and "target" steps) ──────────────────\r\nexport const SCORE_BANDS = [\r\n  { value: \'lte_150\', label: \'150 & below\' },\r\n  { value: \'151_155\', label: \'151–155\' },\r\n  { value: \'156_160\', label: \'156–160\' },\r\n  { value: \'161_165\', label: \'161–165\' },\r\n  { value: \'166_170\', label: \'166–170\' },\r\n  { value: \'171_175\', label: \'171–175\' },\r\n  { value: \'176_180\', label: \'176–180\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type ScoreBand = (typeof SCORE_BANDS)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\n// Per-field extras appended after the shared bands.\r\n// "Haven\'t taken it" is no longer an option here: the taken_lsat step asks that\r\n// first and skips this step entirely, so the question is never put to someone it\r\n// does not apply to. The stored value survives for students who answered it\r\n// under the old flow.\r\nexport const LAST_SCORE_EXTRAS = [\r\n  { value: \'not_sure\', label: "I\'m not sure" },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport const TARGET_SCORE_EXTRAS = [\r\n  { value: \'not_sure\', label: "I\'m not sure yet" },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type LastScoreValue = ScoreBand | \'not_taken\' | \'not_sure\';\r\nexport type TargetScoreValue = ScoreBand | \'not_sure\';\r\n\r\n// ── Life stage (step 5) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\nexport const LIFE_STAGES = [\r\n  { value: \'undergraduate\', title: \'Undergraduate\', desc: "Currently pursuing a bachelor\'s degree" },\r\n  { value: \'graduate\', title: \'Graduate\', desc: \'Pursuing or completed a graduate degree\' },\r\n  { value: \'working\', title: \'Working\', desc: \'Currently employed full or part-time\' },\r\n  { value: \'other\', title: \'All Other Cases\', desc: \'None of the above apply to me\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type LifeStage = (typeof LIFE_STAGES)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\n// ── Study time per day (step 6) ─────────────────────────────────────────────\r\nexport const STUDY_TIME_OPTIONS = [\r\n  { value: \'none\', label: \'No time\' },\r\n  { value: \'lt_1\', label: \'< 1 hr\' },\r\n  { value: \'1_2\', label: \'1–2 hrs\' },\r\n  { value: \'3_plus\', label: \'3+ hrs\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type StudyTimeValue = (typeof STUDY_TIME_OPTIONS)[number][\'value\'];\r\n\r\nexport const DAYS = [\r\n  { key: \'sun\', label: \'Sun\' },\r\n  { key: \'mon\', label: \'Mon\' },\r\n  { key: \'tue\', label: \'Tue\' },\r\n  { key: \'wed\', label: \'Wed\' },\r\n  { key: \'thu\', label: \'Thu\' },\r\n  { key: \'fri\', label: \'Fri\' },\r\n  { key: \'sat\', label: \'Sat\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport type DayKey = (typeof DAYS)[number][\'key\'];\r\n\r\nexport const DEFAULT_STUDY_TIME: Record<DayKey, StudyTimeValue> = {\r\n  sun: \'none\', mon: \'none\', tue: \'none\', wed: \'none\', thu: \'none\', fri: \'none\', sat: \'none\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n// ── Test date (asked right after the target score) ──────────────────────────\r\n// Stored twice on purpose: the ISO date lands in the real `profiles.test_date`\r\n// column (the classroom dateline counts down from it), and a copy lives in the\r\n// answers blob so "have they answered this step?" stays a single read. A\r\n// student who has not registered yet picks UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE, which answers\r\n// the step while leaving the column null — no invented date.\r\nexport const UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE = \'undecided\';\r\n\r\nexport type TestDateValue = string; // \'YYYY-MM-DD\' | typeof UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE\r\n\r\n/** The value written to profiles.test_date: a real date, or null when undecided. */\r\nexport function testDateColumnValue(value: TestDateValue | undefined): string | null {\r\n  if (!value || value === UNDECIDED_TEST_DATE) return null;\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ── The stored answer shape (profiles.onboarding) ───────────────────────────\r\nexport interface OnboardingAnswers {\r\n  has_taken_lsat?: boolean;\r\n  last_score_band?: LastScoreValue;\r\n  experience_text?: string;\r\n  schools?: string[];\r\n  target_score_band?: TargetScoreValue;\r\n  test_date?: TestDateValue;\r\n  life_stage?: LifeStage;\r\n  study_time?: Record<DayKey, StudyTimeValue>;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// The intake steps (name is step 0, handled before these). Order is locked to\r\n// the approved reference, with the test date slotted in after the target score\r\n// so the three "where are you headed" questions sit together.\r\nexport type OnboardingStepId =\r\n  | \'taken_lsat\'\r\n  | \'last_score\'\r\n  | \'experience\'\r\n  | \'schools\'\r\n  | \'target_score\'\r\n  | \'test_date\'\r\n  | \'life_stage\'\r\n  | \'study_time\';\r\n\r\n// Which answer field each step fills. Used to compute the first unanswered step\r\n// on resume. Steps whose field is optional never block resume-completion.\r\nexport const STEP_ORDER: OnboardingStepId[] = [\r\n  \'taken_lsat\',\r\n  \'last_score\',\r\n  \'experience\',\r\n  \'schools\',\r\n  \'target_score\',\r\n  \'test_date\',\r\n  \'life_stage\',\r\n  \'study_time\',\r\n];\r\n\r\n// Required steps gate the "first unanswered step" on resume. Optional steps\r\n// (experience, schools) are skippable within the flow without blocking.\r\nexport const REQUIRED_STEPS: Record<OnboardingStepId, boolean> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: true,\r\n  last_score: true,\r\n  experience: false,\r\n  schools: false,\r\n  target_score: true,\r\n  test_date: true, // answerable as "no date yet", so never a dead end\r\n  life_stage: true,\r\n  study_time: true, // defaulted, so effectively always satisfiable\r\n};\r\n\r\n// ── Copy ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\n// One home for every headline the intake shows, so the card and any future\r\n// surface cannot drift into two versions of the same question.\r\nexport const NAME_STEP_COPY = {\r\n  title: \'What should we call you?\',\r\n  subtitle: \'This is the name your classroom and Joshua will use.\',\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const STEP_COPY: Record<OnboardingStepId, { title: string; subtitle?: string }> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: {\r\n    title: \'Have you taken the LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'An official, scored test. Practice tests do not count here.\',\r\n  },\r\n  last_score: {\r\n    title: \'What did you get on your last LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'This sets your starting point.\',\r\n  },\r\n  experience: {\r\n    title: \'How has your LSAT experience been so far?\',\r\n    subtitle:\r\n      \'Share what has felt easy and what has felt hard so we can personalize your plan.\',\r\n  },\r\n  schools: {\r\n    title: \'What schools are you interested in?\',\r\n    subtitle: "We\'ll build your experience with LSAT U around getting into these schools.",\r\n  },\r\n  target_score: {\r\n    title: \'What score are you aiming for?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'This helps us set the right pace for your prep.\',\r\n  },\r\n  test_date: {\r\n    title: \'When are you taking the LSAT?\',\r\n    subtitle: \'Your classroom counts down from this and paces your week around it.\',\r\n  },\r\n  life_stage: {\r\n    title: \'Which best describes you?\',\r\n  },\r\n  study_time: {\r\n    title: \'Build your study week\',\r\n    subtitle:\r\n      \'Show Joshua when LSAT prep can realistically fit. Start with the days you have, then choose the time you can protect on each one.\',\r\n  },\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Short row labels for the Account page, where the answers are listed rather\r\n// than asked. The full questions live in STEP_COPY and stay the card\'s voice.\r\nexport const STEP_LABELS: Record<OnboardingStepId, string> = {\r\n  taken_lsat: \'Taken the LSAT\',\r\n  last_score: \'Last LSAT score\',\r\n  experience: \'How it has gone so far\',\r\n  schools: \'Target schools\',\r\n  target_score: \'Target score\',\r\n  test_date: \'Test date\',\r\n  life_stage: \'Where you are at\',\r\n  study_time: \'Weekly study time\',\r\n};\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The steps this student is actually asked, in order. Answering "no" to\r\n * taken_lsat drops the score step rather than showing it with an escape hatch,\r\n * which also keeps the "Step N of M" counter honest.\r\n */\r\nexport function visibleSteps(a: OnboardingAnswers): OnboardingStepId[] {\r\n  return STEP_ORDER.filter((step) => step !== \'last_score\' || a.has_taken_lsat !== false);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Whether a given step\'s answer is present. Drives resume and the Continue gate. */\r\nexport function isStepAnswered(step: OnboardingStepId, a: OnboardingAnswers): boolean {\r\n  switch (step) {\r\n    case \'taken_lsat\':\r\n      return typeof a.has_taken_lsat === \'boolean\';\r\n    case \'last_score\':\r\n      return !!a.last_score_band;\r\n    case \'experience\':\r\n      return !!a.experience_text?.trim();\r\n    case \'schools\':\r\n      return !!a.schools && a.schools.length > 0;\r\n    case \'target_score\':\r\n      return !!a.target_score_band;\r\n    case \'test_date\':\r\n      return !!a.test_date;\r\n    case \'life_stage\':\r\n      return !!a.life_stage;\r\n    case \'study_time\':\r\n      return !!a.study_time;\r\n    default:\r\n      return false;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n'}]
