A crash between the\r\n// two leaves a \'pending\' row the reconciler adopts; the booking is honored.\r\n//\r\n// The GoogleCalendar dependency is injected so tests can supply a deterministic\r\n// mock and exercise the crash matrix without touching Google.\r\n\r\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "./sendAppEmail.ts";\r\n\r\n\r\nexport const BOOKING_CFG = {\r\n  sessionMinDefault: 70,\r\n  bufferMin: 15,\r\n  stepMin: 30,\r\n  leadMin: 1440,\r\n  lookaheadDaysDefault: 21,\r\n  maxSlots: 200,\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport type BookingPass = {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  status: string;\r\n  total_credits: number;\r\n  used_credits: number;\r\n  session_length_minutes: number;\r\n  expires_at: string | null;\r\n  student_id: string | null;\r\n  student_name: string | null;\r\n  student_email: string | null;\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport type BookingIdentity = {\r\n  student_id: string | null; // null for name/email-only passes (legacy); slug passes carry it\r\n  email: string | null;\r\n  name: string;\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport type GoogleCal = {\r\n  insertEvent: (calendarId: string, body: unknown, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;\r\n  patchEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string, body: unknown, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;\r\n  getEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string) => Promise<any>;\r\n  deleteEvent: (calendarId: string, eventId: string, opts?: any) => Promise<any>;\r\n  freeBusy: (calIds: string[], startIso: string, endIso: string) => Promise<Record<string, { start: string; end: string }[]>>;\r\n  meetUrlFromEvent: (event: any) => string | null;\r\n};\r\n\r\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- timezone utils\r\n// UTC offset (minutes) of an IANA tz at an instant (DST-aware; positive = east).\r\nfunction tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone: string, at: Date): number {\r\n  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone, hourCycle: "h23",\r\n    year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",\r\n    hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",\r\n  });\r\n  const map: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) if (p.type !== "literal") map[p.type] = Number(p.value);\r\n  const asUtc = Date.UTC(map.year, map.month - 1, map.day, map.hour, map.minute, map.second);\r\n  return Math.round((asUtc - at.getTime()) / 60000);\r\n}\r\n\r\n// UTC instant for a host-local calendar date + wall-clock minutes (settles DST).\r\nfunction hostLocalToUtc(timeZone: string, y: number, m: number, d: number, minutesOfDay: number): Date {\r\n  const naiveUtc = Date.UTC(y, m, d, Math.floor(minutesOfDay / 60), minutesOfDay % 60);\r\n  let guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, new Date(naiveUtc)) * 60000);\r\n  guess = new Date(naiveUtc - tzOffsetMinutesAt(timeZone, guess) * 60000);\r\n  return guess;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction hostLocalParts(timeZone: string, at: Date) {\r\n  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit", weekday: "short",\r\n  });\r\n  const map: Record<string, string> = {};\r\n  for (const p of dtf.formatToParts(at)) map[p.type] = p.value;\r\n  const wd: Record<string, number> = { Sun: 0, Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6 };\r\n  return { y: Number(map.year), m: Number(map.month) - 1, d: Number(map.day), dow: wd[map.weekday] };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type Slot = { start_at: string; end_at: string };\r\n\r\nexport async function loadBusyIntervals(opts: {\r\n  supabase: any;\r\n  gcal: GoogleCal;\r\n  rangeStartIso: string;\r\n  rangeEndIso: string;\r\n}): Promise<{ start: number; end: number }[]> {\r\n  const { supabase, gcal, rangeStartIso, rangeEndIso } = opts;\r\n  const { data: state, error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")\r\n    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (stateErr) throw stateErr;\r\n  const writeCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n  const calendarIds = [...new Set([writeCalendar, ...((state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? []) as string[])])];\r\n  const { data: mirrored, error: mirrorErr } = await supabase.from("schedule_events")\r\n    .select("start_at,end_at")\r\n    .is("deleted_at", null).neq("status", "cancelled").neq("transparency", "transparent")\r\n    .lt("start_at", rangeEndIso).gt("end_at", rangeStartIso);\r\n  if (mirrorErr) throw mirrorErr;\r\n  // Native Single Session Holds are first-class occupancy. Google normally\r\n  // sees the tentative event too, but the database union closes the cache and\r\n  // propagation window and keeps package slot browsing symmetric.\r\n  const { data: singleSessions, error: singleSessionErr } = await supabase\r\n    .from("single_session_bookings")\r\n    .select("start_at,end_at")\r\n    .in("status", ["draft", "checkout_open", "booked"])\r\n    .lt("start_at", rangeEndIso)\r\n    .gt("end_at", rangeStartIso);\r\n  if (singleSessionErr && singleSessionErr.code !== "42P01") throw singleSessionErr;\r\n  const live = await gcal.freeBusy(calendarIds, rangeStartIso, rangeEndIso);\r\n  const intervals = [\r\n    ...(mirrored ?? []).map((e: any) => ({ start: new Date(e.start_at).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end_at).getTime() })),\r\n    ...(singleSessions ?? []).map((e: any) => ({ start: new Date(e.start_at).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end_at).getTime() })),\r\n    ...Object.values(live).flat().map((e) => ({ start: new Date(e.start).getTime(), end: new Date(e.end).getTime() })),\r\n  ].filter((e) => Number.isFinite(e.start) && Number.isFinite(e.end));\r\n  return intervals;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function deterministicGoogleEventId(redemptionId: string): string {\r\n  return redemptionId.replace(/-/g, "").toLowerCase();\r\n}\r\n\r\n// Generate bookable slots: 30-min grid, inside seeded availability windows for\r\n// the host tz, >= 24h lead, session must fit fully, 15-min buffer around busy\r\n// blocks. `busy` = existing events (ms epoch). This mirrors the server-side\r\n// is_slot_bookable predicate so what students see is what the RPC will accept.\r\nexport function generateSlots(opts: {\r\n  pass: BookingPass;\r\n  nowMs: number;\r\n  hostTz: string;\r\n  rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]>;\r\n  busy: { start: number; end: number }[];\r\n  daysAhead: number;\r\n}): Slot[] {\r\n  const { pass, nowMs, hostTz, rangesByDow, busy, daysAhead } = opts;\r\n  const sessionMs = pass.session_length_minutes * 60_000;\r\n  const bufMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;\r\n  const rangeStart = nowMs + BOOKING_CFG.leadMin * 60_000;\r\n  let rangeEnd = nowMs + daysAhead * 86_400_000;\r\n  if (pass.expires_at) {\r\n    const exp = new Date(pass.expires_at).getTime();\r\n    if (exp < rangeEnd) rangeEnd = exp;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const slots: Slot[] = [];\r\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\r\n  for (let d = 0; d <= daysAhead && slots.length < BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots; d++) {\r\n    const dayAnchor = new Date(rangeStart + d * 86_400_000);\r\n    const { y, m, d: dd, dow } = hostLocalParts(hostTz, dayAnchor);\r\n    const dayRanges = rangesByDow[dow] ?? [];\r\n    for (const rng of dayRanges) {\r\n      // Step on the 30-min grid; require the whole session to fit in the range.\r\n      const firstGridMinute = Math.ceil(rng.startMin / BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) * BOOKING_CFG.stepMin;\r\n      for (let mins = firstGridMinute; mins + pass.session_length_minutes <= rng.endMin; mins += BOOKING_CFG.stepMin) {\r\n        const startUtc = hostLocalToUtc(hostTz, y, m, dd, mins).getTime();\r\n        if (startUtc < rangeStart) continue;\r\n        if (startUtc + sessionMs > rangeEnd) break;\r\n        // 15-min buffer around each busy block.\r\n        const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start - bufMs < startUtc + sessionMs && b.end + bufMs > startUtc);\r\n        if (!conflict) {\r\n          const startIso = new Date(startUtc).toISOString();\r\n          if (!seen.has(startIso)) {\r\n            seen.add(startIso);\r\n            slots.push({ start_at: startIso, end_at: new Date(startUtc + sessionMs).toISOString() });\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n        if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;\r\n      }\r\n      if (slots.length >= BOOKING_CFG.maxSlots) break;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  slots.sort((a, b) => a.start_at.localeCompare(b.start_at));\r\n  return slots;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// Parse the get_booking_availability() RPC payload into rangesByDow + tz.\r\nexport function parseAvailability(avail: any): { hostTz: string; rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]> } {\r\n  const rangesByDow: Record<number, { startMin: number; endMin: number }[]> = {};\r\n  let hostTz = "America/Toronto";\r\n  if (avail?.timezone) hostTz = avail.timezone;\r\n  for (const r of (avail?.ranges ?? []) as any[]) {\r\n    const [sh, sm] = String(r.start_time).split(":").map(Number);\r\n    const [eh, em] = String(r.end_time).split(":").map(Number);\r\n    (rangesByDow[r.day_of_week] ??= []).push({ startMin: sh * 60 + sm, endMin: eh * 60 + em });\r\n  }\r\n  return { hostTz, rangesByDow };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type BookResult =\r\n  | { ok: true; redemption: any; was_existing: boolean }\r\n  | { ok: false; error: string; status: number; details?: string };\r\n\r\n// Reserve-first booking. `supabase` is a service-role client. `gcal` is injected.\r\nexport async function bookSession(opts: {\r\n  supabase: any;\r\n  gcal: GoogleCal;\r\n  pass: BookingPass;\r\n  identity: BookingIdentity;\r\n  startAtIso: string;\r\n  idempotencyKey: string;\r\n  adminEmail: string;\r\n  supabaseUrl: string;\r\n  serviceKey: string;\r\n  anonKey: string;\r\n  studentNote?: string;\r\n}): Promise<BookResult> {\r\n  const { supabase, gcal, pass, identity, startAtIso, idempotencyKey: idem } = opts;\r\n  const studentNote = (opts.studentNote || "").trim();\r\n\r\n  const startAt = new Date(startAtIso);\r\n  if (isNaN(startAt.getTime())) return { ok: false, error: "invalid_start_at", status: 400 };\r\n  if (!idem || idem.length < 8) return { ok: false, error: "invalid_idempotency_key", status: 400 };\r\n  const endAt = new Date(startAt.getTime() + pass.session_length_minutes * 60_000);\r\n  const studentName = identity.name;\r\n  const studentEmail = identity.email;\r\n\r\n  // Freshness guard immediately before the database reservation. The\r\n  // exclusion constraint remains the hard guard for LSAT U races.\r\n  try {\r\n    const bufferMs = BOOKING_CFG.bufferMin * 60_000;\r\n    const busy = await loadBusyIntervals({\r\n      supabase,\r\n      gcal,\r\n      rangeStartIso: new Date(startAt.getTime() - bufferMs).toISOString(),\r\n      rangeEndIso: new Date(endAt.getTime() + bufferMs).toISOString(),\r\n    });\r\n    const conflict = busy.some((b) => b.start - bufferMs < endAt.getTime() && b.end + bufferMs > startAt.getTime());\r\n    if (conflict) return { ok: false, error: "slot_conflict", status: 409 };\r\n  } catch (busyError) {\r\n    return {\r\n      ok: false,\r\n      error: "availability_check_failed",\r\n      status: 503,\r\n      details: busyError instanceof Error ? busyError.message : String(busyError),\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ---- STEP 1: RESERVE (in-txn slot policy + credit debit, gcal_state=pending)\r\n  const { data: rpc, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc("redeem_booking_pass_v2", {\r\n    _pass_id: pass.id,\r\n    _idempotency_key: idem,\r\n    _start_at: startAt.toISOString(),\r\n    _schedule_event_id: null,\r\n    _student_name: studentName,\r\n    _student_email: studentEmail,\r\n  });\r\n  if (rpcErr) {\r\n    const msg = String(rpcErr.message || "");\r\n    const code = rpcErr.code as string | undefined;\r\n    const mapped =\r\n      code === "P0425" || msg.includes("slot_unavailable") ? "slot_unavailable"\r\n      : code === "P0409" || msg.includes("slot_conflict") || msg.includes("no_overlapping_bookings") ? "slot_conflict"\r\n      : msg.includes("pass_exhausted") ? "pass_exhausted"\r\n      : msg.includes("pass_expired") ? "pass_expired"\r\n      : msg.includes("pass_not_active") ? "pass_not_active"\r\n      : "redeem_failed";\r\n    return { ok: false, error: mapped, status: 409, details: msg };\r\n  }\r\n  const row = Array.isArray(rpc) ? rpc[0] : rpc;\r\n  const redemptionId = row?.redemption_id;\r\n  if (row?.was_existing) {\r\n    // Idempotent replay: return the already-booked row.\r\n    const { data: existing } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions").select("*").eq("id", redemptionId).maybeSingle();\r\n    return { ok: true, redemption: existing, was_existing: true };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ---- Booking-time student submission: persist the note + link any files\r\n  // staged (by booking-upload) under this (pass, idempotency_key). Done before\r\n  // gcal so a Google failure never loses the student\'s note/attachments.\r\n  let uploadedFileNames: string[] = [];\r\n  let submissionStatus: "none" | "linked" | "failed" = "none";\r\n  try {\r\n    if (studentNote) {\r\n      const { error: noteErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n        .update({ student_note: studentNote }).eq("id", redemptionId);\r\n      if (noteErr) throw noteErr;\r\n    }\r\n    const { data: linked, error: linkErr } = await supabase.from("external_work_uploads")\r\n      .update({ redemption_id: redemptionId, student_id: identity.student_id ?? undefined })\r\n      .eq("booking_pass_id", pass.id)\r\n      .eq("booking_idempotency_key", idem)\r\n      .is("redemption_id", null)\r\n      .select("file_name");\r\n    if (linkErr) throw linkErr;\r\n    uploadedFileNames = (linked ?? []).map((r: any) => r.file_name).filter(Boolean);\r\n    if (studentNote || uploadedFileNames.length > 0) {\r\n      submissionStatus = "linked";\r\n      const { error: statusErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n        .update({ submission_status: "linked", submission_error: null }).eq("id", redemptionId);\r\n      if (statusErr) throw statusErr;\r\n    }\r\n  } catch (subErr) {\r\n    submissionStatus = "failed";\r\n    const message = subErr instanceof Error ? subErr.message : String(subErr);\r\n    console.error("linking booking submission failed", { redemptionId, error: message });\r\n    const { error: repairErr } = await supabase.from("booking_pass_redemptions")\r\n      .update({ submission_status: "failed", submission_error: message }).eq("id", redemptionId);\r\n    if (repairErr) console.error("submission repair state failed", repairErr);\r\n    const { error: alertErr } = await supabase.from("booking_calendar_alerts").insert({\r\n      redemption_id: redemptionId, alert_type: "persistence_failure",\r\n      details: { category: "submission_linkage", error: message },\r\n    });\r\n    if (alertErr && alertErr.code !== "23505") console.error("submission repair alert failed", alertErr);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Resolve the target calendar.\r\n  const { data: stateRow, error: stateErr } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (stateErr) {\r\n    return { ok: false, error: "calendar_config_failed", status: 503, details: stateErr.message };\r\n  }\r\n  const calendarId = stateRow?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n\r\n  // Calendar event title: "<first name> session" (e.g. "Ashley session").\r\n  const firstName = String(studentName).trim().split(/\\s+/)[0] || String(studentName).trim();\r\n  const eventTitle = `${firstName} session`;\r\n\r\n  // Sessions the student has left AFTER this booking — stated plainly, no\r\n  // "X of Y" / "remaining" pressure framing.\r\n  const creditsLeft = Math.max(0, (pass.total_credits ?? 0) - (pass.used_credits + 1));\r\n  const description = [\r\n    "Booked via LSAT U.",\r\n    studentEmail ? `Student: ${studentName} <${studentEmail}>` : `Student: ${studentName}`,\r\n    `${creditsLeft} session${creditsLeft === 1 ? "" : "s"} in your package`,\r\n  ].join("\\n");\r\n\r\n  // ---- STEP 2: GOOGLE (event + Meet, stamped for the reconciler)\r\n  let gEvent: any;\r\n  const deterministicEventId = deterministicGoogleEventId(redemptionId);\r\n  try {\r\n    gEvent = await gcal.insertEvent(calendarId, {\r\n      id: deterministicEventId,\r\n      summary: eventTitle,\r\n      description,\r\n      start: { dateTime: startAt.toISOString() },\r\n      end: { dateTime: endAt.toISOString() },\r\n      conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: idem, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },\r\n      attendees: studentEmail ? [{ email: studentEmail }] : undefined,\r\n      // The stamp the reconciler searches by to adopt-or-create idempotently.\r\n      extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_redemption_id: redemptionId, lsatu_idempotency_key: idem, lsatu_pass_id: pass.id } },\r\n    }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: studentEmail ? "all" : "none" });\r\n  } catch (gErr) {\r\n    if ((gErr as { status?: number }).status === 409) {\r\n      try {\r\n        gEvent = await gcal.getEvent(calendarId, deterministicEventId);\r\n      } catch (adoptErr) {\r\n        console.error("google event conflict could not be adopted", adoptErr);\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    if (gEvent) {\r\n      // Adopted the deterministic event created by an earlier timed-out call.\r\n    } else {\r\n    // Booking is RESERVED and honored. Leave the row \'pending\' for the\r\n    // reconciler; do NOT roll back a paid slot. Report success with pending sync.\r\n    console.error("google insert failed; left pending for reconciler", gErr);\r\n    return {\r\n      ok: true,\r\n      was_existing: false,\r\n      redemption: {\r\n        id: redemptionId, start_at: startAt.toISOString(), end_at: endAt.toISOString(),\r\n        status: "booked", gcal_state: "pending", meet_url: null, google_event_id: null,\r\n        submission_status: submissionStatus,\r\n      },\r\n    };\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  let meetUrl = gcal.meetUrlFromEvent(gEvent);\r\n  if (!meetUrl) {\r\n    try {\r\n      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(…6409 tokens truncated…ed," "generated vs adapted." Admin side keeps full\r\n  source metadata for trust/debugging.\r\n- Only enrolled/current tutoring students enter the pipeline. Consultation notes do\r\n  not auto-become intelligence records unless that person later enrolls and the\r\n  record is intentionally connected.\r\n- Student matching requires **email AND name**, never name alone. Uncertain → review\r\n  queue; publish/assign/message nothing until resolved.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 2. End-to-end flow (V1, manual trigger)\r\n\r\nJoshua clicks **Scan for new session notes** in the Teacher Workspace. The\r\norchestrator runs:\r\n\r\n1. **Detect** — `gmail` edge function searches for Gemini Notes emails since the last\r\n   scan. Subject pattern looks like `Notes: "Lydia" Jun 19, 2026`. The email carries\r\n   a Google Doc link.\r\n2. **Fetch** — parse the Doc link; `gdrive` edge function exports the Doc (it is the\r\n   richer source: summary, next steps, details, invited student email, full\r\n   timestamped transcript).\r\n3. **Verify** — corroborate against the Google Calendar event (time, attendees) using\r\n   the already-synced `schedule_events` / `gcal-list-calendars`.\r\n4. **Match** — resolve to an enrolled student by **email AND name** (§3).\r\n5. **Store source** — store the raw transcript (§5a) and create/link a\r\n   `student_sessions` row (`scheduled_at`, `duration_minutes`, `status`,\r\n   `transcript_id`, `source=\'gemini_notes\'`, `created_by`).\r\n6. **Synthesize** — AI builds the bundle (§4) and writes it to `ai_ta_drafts` as a\r\n   `classroom_update_bundle` (admin-only). **Phase 2 — not in Phase 1.**\r\n7. **Queue** — the item appears in the **review queue**. Nothing is student-visible.\r\n\r\nEvery step writes to a `session_ingestions` ledger row (§9) for idempotency and\r\nresolution-queue state.\r\n\r\n> **Phase boundary:** Steps 1–5 are Phase 1 (1A = manual single ingest from a known\r\n> Gmail/Doc id; 1B = scan window + resolution queue + calendar corroboration). Step 6\r\n> (synthesis) is Phase 2. Publish is Phase 3. See `claude-implementation-plan.md`.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 3. Matching & resolution\r\n\r\n### 3a. The "matchable student" predicate (exact, from repo reality)\r\n\r\nThere is no generic "enrolled" flag. The roster comes from the `admin-manage-users`\r\nedge function via `useHubStudents` (`HubStudentRow`), keyed by **`class_id`**, carrying\r\n`role`, `email`, `display_name`, and granular `has_*_access` flags. The matchable set is:\r\n\r\n> A `HubStudentRow` where **`role = \'student\'`** (i.e. not `\'admin\'`) **AND\r\n> `has_classroom_access = true`** (this is the flag that gates whether the student can\r\n> receive `classroom_items` at all, so it is the correct delivery gate). The publish\r\n> step additionally requires **`has_chat_access = true`** for the inbox/chat message; if\r\n> a matched student lacks it, the note/homework can still publish but the chat message\r\n> is skipped and reported truthfully (not faked).\r\n\r\nAnyone not in this set (admins, consultations, access-revoked profiles) is **not**\r\nmatchable and falls to the resolution queue.\r\n\r\n### 3b. ID resolution (class_id → auth.users.id) — RESOLVED\r\n\r\nThe roster is keyed by `class_id`, but every storage/delivery table the pipeline writes\r\nto keys off **`auth.users(id)`**: `student_context.student_id`,\r\n`student_sessions.student_id`, `ai_ta_drafts.student_id`, `notifications.user_id`,\r\n`conversation_participants.user_id`.\r\n\r\n**Resolved by repo inspection (not assumed):** `profiles.class_id` **is** the\r\n`auth.users.id` — the same UUID, stored as `text` (`profiles.class_id text PRIMARY KEY`;\r\n`auth.users.id uuid`). Evidence in `supabase/functions/admin-manage-users/index.ts`:\r\nemail is resolved as `emailMap.get(p.class_id)` where `emailMap` is keyed by\r\n`auth.users.id` (lines ~132/142), and role as `roleMap.get(p.class_id)` keyed by\r\n`user_roles.user_id` (~137/143). Existing app code already passes `class_id` directly\r\ninto the uuid FK columns (e.g. `ai_ta_drafts.student_id` via `useAITADrafts`), relying on\r\nPostgres `text`→`uuid` casting.\r\n\r\n**The safe resolver** (build once, reuse everywhere — not a raw cast):\r\n\r\n```\r\nresolveStudentAuthId(class_id: string): uuid | null\r\n  1. Trim. Reject if not a well-formed UUID (regex/`::uuid` guard) → return null.\r\n  2. Confirm a matching auth.users row exists (admin client\r\n     `admin.auth.admin.getUserById(class_id)` OR a profiles+auth join). \r\n  3. Return the uuid on success.\r\n  4. Orphan/invalid (the `email === \'unknown\'` case useHubStudents already logs) →\r\n     return null. Caller must NOT write; route to the resolution queue with\r\n     match_reason=\'unresolved_auth_id\'.\r\n```\r\n\r\n**Fail closed:** no `student_context` / `student_sessions` / `ai_ta_drafts` write happens\r\nagainst an unresolved or orphan id. This resolver is a Phase 1A deliverable (a small\r\nshared helper, e.g. `src/lib/resolveStudentAuthId.ts` + the same guard in the edge\r\nfunction), used by every phase.\r\n\r\n### 3c. Match decision\r\n\r\n- **Confident match**: the Doc\'s invited-student email matches a matchable student\'s\r\n  `email` (case-insensitive) **AND** the name agrees (normalized compare of the Gemini\r\n  subject name vs `display_name`) **AND** calendar corroborates (§3d) → auto-attach,\r\n  `match_confidence=\'high\'`, record `match_reason`.\r\n- **Uncertain / missing**: any disagreement, missing email, name-only signal, or no\r\n  calendar corroboration → `status=\'needs_match\'`, into the **resolution queue**. Admin\r\n  picks the correct student or discards (`discard_reason`). No transcript is attached to\r\n  a student until resolved.\r\n- **Idempotency**: dedup by unique `gmail_message_id` and unique `gdrive_doc_id` so\r\n  re-scans never double-ingest. The `session_ingestions` ledger is the dedup key (§9).\r\n\r\n### 3d. Calendar corroboration (claim corrected)\r\n\r\n`gcal-sync-pull` writes `schedule_events`, but that table stores only\r\n`title/description/location/start_at/end_at/html_link/status` — **no attendee emails**.\r\nSo attendee-email corroboration is **not** available from the synced table. Two options,\r\ndecided per phase:\r\n\r\n- **V1 (Phase 1B):** corroborate by **time window + title only** — find a\r\n  `schedule_events` row whose `start_at` is near the session time and whose title\r\n  contains the student name. This is a soft signal that *raises* confidence but cannot\r\n  *confirm* identity by itself; identity still rests on email+name from the Doc.\r\n- **If attendee-email corroboration is required:** add a **direct Google Calendar event\r\n  fetch** at scan time (new `gcal-event-get` call returning `attendees[].email`), or\r\n  extend `gcal-sync-pull` + `schedule_events` to persist attendees. This is an explicit\r\n  added scope item, flagged in `lovable-handoff-prompt.md`, **not** assumed present.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 4. Synthesis output (the bundle)\r\n\r\nOne `ai_ta_drafts` row of `draft_type=\'classroom_update_bundle\'`. `draft_json` holds\r\nthe parts; each part is independently editable and approvable:\r\n\r\n- Polished session note — clean, Notion-style sections + checklists, concise.\r\n- Homework checklist.\r\n- Quiz / mini-drill — **prefer existing LSAT U material** via question-bank refs; may\r\n  generate low-stakes recall or concept checks when the transcript supports it.\r\n  Source metadata (existing-vs-generated) preserved internally, never shown to student.\r\n- Mistake-pattern checklist.\r\n- Key-concept cards.\r\n- Weakness tags.\r\n- Drill recommendations.\r\n- Next-session agenda.\r\n- Private tutor/admin note (admin-only, never published).\r\n\r\nAll student-facing copy is routed through the teaching-voice guidance\r\n(`~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/wiki/concepts/teaching-voice.md`). V1 generates a part\r\n**only when the transcript clearly supports it** — no forced sections.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 5a. Transcript storage (decided)\r\n\r\n**The `student-context` bucket is the canonical raw-transcript store. Always.** The\r\nexported Doc (full transcript as a text file, plus optionally the PDF/HTML export) is\r\nwritten to the bucket and referenced by `student_context.file_path`. This is the source\r\nof record and holds the complete transcript regardless of size.\r\n\r\n**`content_text` is a convenience copy, only when small.** Store the transcript text in\r\n`student_context.content_text` **only if it is under ~100 KB**, so the synthesis step and\r\nTA prompt builders can read it inline without a fetch.\r\n\r\n- **≤ ~100 KB:** write the full text to `content_text` **and** the canonical file to the\r\n  bucket. `metadata.inline_text=true`.\r\n- **> ~100 KB:** bucket only. Leave `content_text` null (or a short header excerpt),\r\n  set `metadata.inline_text=false` and `metadata.full_artifact_path` = the bucket object.\r\n  Synthesis reads the bucket object (chunked) in this case.\r\n\r\nNever rely on `content_text` as the source of truth — the bucket object is canonical.\r\nThe `student_sessions.transcript_id` FK points at this `student_context` row.\r\n\r\n## 5. Admin review workflow\r\n\r\nReuse `ai_ta_drafts` + `useAITADrafts` / `useUpdateDraftStatus`. Per-part edit and\r\napprove.\r\n\r\n**Status rollup (corrected — `ai_ta_drafts.status` only has\r\n`draft/approved/rejected/posted`):** per-part approval lives in `draft_json` (each part\r\ncarries its own `{ approved: boolean, edited_at, ... }`). The **row\'s `status` stays\r\n`draft`** while any part is unapproved. The row only moves to `approved` when **every\r\nincluded part is approved** (or explicitly excluded). Never mark the whole row\r\n`approved` because one part was approved — the per-part flags in `draft_json` are the\r\nsource of truth; `status` is a coarse rollup used only by existing list/filter UI.\r\n`rejected` applies to the whole row (discard the bundle). `posted` is set by the publish\r\nstep (§6) once at least one approved part has been materialized. Approve ≠ publish.\r\n\r\nThe seven separately approvable items match the brief: student-facing note, homework\r\nchecklist, quiz/mini-drill, weakness tags, drill recommendations, next-session agenda,\r\nprivate tutor/admin note.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 6. Publish (separate action — decision 4)\r\n\r\nA `publish-session-bundle` step materializes **only approved parts**:\r\n\r\n- Note → `classroom_items` (and/or recap via `RecapComposer` path).\r\n- Homework → `homework_assignments`.\r\n- Quiz / drill → assignment (homework or guided-drill assignment).\r\n\r\n**Notification + chat copy (corrected).** The existing helper\r\n`notifyStudentOfClassroomItem` (`src/lib/classroomNotify.ts`) hardcodes\r\n`"New assignment: …"` copy across the inbox body, the bell `notifications.title`, and\r\nthe `send-assignment-email` body (`assignmentType` default `"assignment"`). It is\r\ntherefore correct **only for the homework/assignment parts**, and we reuse it **as-is\r\nfor those**. For the **session note** and the **polished Joshua chat message** we do\r\n**not** route through it (it would mislabel a note as an assignment). Instead:\r\n\r\n- **Phase 3 builds a session-bundle notifier** that reuses the thread-find/create logic\r\n  (factor `findOrCreateThread` out of `classroomNotify.ts`, or call\r\n  `adminConversation.ts`) and the `notifications` + email insert pattern, but with\r\n  **session-note copy** and `notifications.type` appropriate to a note (e.g. add/use a\r\n  `recap`/`note` type rather than `assignment`). The polished Joshua chat message body\r\n  comes from the approved `draft_json.message` part — its copy is authored/edited in\r\n  review, **not** generated by the helper. Until that notifier + copy path is built, the\r\n  spec does **not** promise polished chat copy via the existing helper.\r\n- Partial-success is surfaced truthfully (reuse the `NotifyResult` pattern), never faked.\r\n\r\nSets the draft `status=\'posted\'`, stamps `posted_at`, once at least one approved part is\r\nmaterialized. Idempotent (re-publish skips already-materialized parts; track per-part\r\n`posted` in `draft_json`). Partial publish allowed (publish the note now, hold the\r\nhomework).\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 7. AI Suggestions (admin-only in V1)\r\n\r\nSurfaced in the dossier / desk only. May suggest next steps, homework/drills, note\r\nedits, quiz items, next-session agenda, and history-based insights. Nothing publishes,\r\nassigns, or messages the student without Joshua\'s approval. Long-term path: admin-first\r\n"AI Joshua." Student-facing AI comes only after quality and safety are proven.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 8. Student dossier (tab shell, real tabs only — decision 3)\r\n\r\nConvert `src/pages/teacher/TeacherStudentDetail.tsx` into a tabbed shell. Reuse\r\n`HubStudentList` for the roster.\r\n\r\n**V1 live tabs (data-backed only):**\r\n- **Overview** — current stat cards + most recent session.\r\n- **Sessions / Transcripts** — required. `student_sessions` list + linked transcript\r\n  (admin-only). This is the home of the pipeline\'s per-student output.\r\n- **Homework** — `useStudentClassroomItems` / assignments.\r\n- **Notes** — `student_context` notes + recaps.\r\n\r\n**Designed but deferred** (added only when backed by real data, to avoid placeholder\r\nUI): Analytics/Performance, Schedule, AI Suggestions, Insights. Tab structure is kept\r\nflexible; Sessions/Transcripts is the only mandated tab.\r\n\r\n**Acceptance rule (real-tabs-only):** every V1 tab MUST issue a real query against a\r\nreal table and render an **honest empty state** when there is no data (e.g. "No\r\nsessions logged yet"), never a placeholder or mock. A tab may ship in V1 **only if** it\r\nhas a real data source wired. No Analytics, Schedule, AI Suggestions, or Insights tab\r\nappears in V1 until it queries real data. A PR adding a tab that returns hardcoded/mock\r\ncontent fails review.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 9. Data model proposal\r\n\r\n**Reuse as-is:** `student_sessions`, `student_context`, `ai_ta_drafts`,\r\n`homework_assignments`, `classroom_items`, `conversations` / `messages`,\r\n`notifications`.\r\n\r\n### 9a. RLS / service-role / audit story (corrected — no hand-waving)\r\n\r\nThe existing admin-only tables are **not** simply `using(has_role(...,\'admin\'))`.\r\n`student_context` and `student_sessions` require, on INSERT, **both**\r\n`has_role(auth.uid(),\'admin\')` **AND `created_by = auth.uid()`**. That has a concrete\r\nconsequence for an edge function:\r\n\r\n- **If the orchestrator runs with the admin\'s JWT** (manual Scan, Phase 1A/1B), then\r\n  `auth.uid()` is Joshua, and the function sets `created_by = auth.uid()` — the existing\r\n  RLS is satisfied directly. **This is the V1 path.**\r\n- **If the orchestrator runs with the service role** (future cron, Phase 3), RLS is\r\n  bypassed, so the function MUST set `created_by` explicitly to the **admin actor\'s\r\n  user id** (resolved from the cron config / a designated admin), and MUST stamp the\r\n  ledger\'s `scan_requested_by` so every row is attributable. Service-role writes that\r\n  leave `created_by`/`scan_requested_by` null are a review failure.\r\n\r\n`ai_ta_drafts` insert requires `created_by` too (set the same way).\r\n`session_ingestions` carries its own `created_by` / `scan_requested_by`, `resolved_by`,\r\n`resolved_at` so the whole chain (who scanned, who resolved a match, who discarded) is\r\naudited. Cron compatibility: the `session_ingestions` policy allows admin via\r\n`has_role`, and service-role bypasses RLS but is still required to populate the\r\nattribution columns.\r\n\r\n### 9b. `session_ingestions` ledger (net-new — proposed, NOT created this pass)\r\n\r\nMinimal but with the required safety fields:\r\n\r\n| Column | Type / note |\r\n|--------|-------------|\r\n| `id` | uuid PK |\r\n| `gmail_message_id` | text **UNIQUE** (dedup) |\r\n| `gdrive_doc_id` | text **UNIQUE** (dedup) |\r\n| `calendar_event_id` | text **nullable** (corroboration ref) |\r\n| `status` | text enum: `ingested` / `needs_match` / `matched` / `stored` / `drafted` / `published` / `discarded` / `error` |\r\n| `matched_student_id` | uuid **nullable** → `auth.users(id)` (set on resolve) |\r\n| `match_confidence` | text/enum: `high` / `medium` / `low` / `none` |\r\n| `match_reason` | text (why this match / why uncertain) |\r\n| `transcript_context_id` | uuid **nullable** → `student_context(id)` |\r\n| `draft_id` | uuid **nullable** → `ai_ta_drafts(id)` |\r\n| `raw_subject` | text |\r\n| `raw_meta` | jsonb (sender, doc title, parsed name/email, calendar window hit) |\r\n| `error_message` | text **nullable** |\r\n| `attempt_count` | int default 0 |\r\n| `last_attempt_at` | timestamptz **nullable** |\r\n| `scan_requested_by` | uuid → `auth.users(id)` (who triggered the scan) |\r\n| `created_by` | uuid → `auth.users(id)` (actor for the row) |\r\n| `resolved_by` | uuid **nullable** → `auth.users(id)` |\r\n| `resolved_at` | timestamptz **nullable** |\r\n| `discard_reason` | text **nullable** |\r\n| `created_at` / `updated_at` | timestamptz |\r\n\r\nRLS: admin-only via `has_role(auth.uid(),\'admin\')` for `all`; INSERT additionally\r\nchecks `created_by = auth.uid()` under JWT. Service role bypasses RLS for cron but must\r\npopulate `created_by` + `scan_requested_by` (§9a). Unique constraints on\r\n`gmail_message_id` and `gdrive_doc_id` are the idempotency guard. This table is the\r\ndedup key **and** the backing store for the resolution queue.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 10. Claude-direct vs Lovable/Supabase-dependent\r\n\r\n**Already built / applied:**\r\n- `session_ingestions` migration was applied via Lovable as\r\n  `supabase/migrations/20260624204004_2f2c47bc-32b9-4cf0-bb39-43b29e9fd7c4.sql`.\r\n- Phase 1A `ingest-session-note` and `/teacher/session-ingest` were built and initially\r\n  deployed.\r\n- Phase 1B `scan-session-notes`, shared ingest core, hook, scan button, and resolution\r\n  queue UI are code complete on `main`.\r\n\r\n**Still needs Joshua/Lovable:**\r\n- Redeploy the frontend and `ingest-session-note` from current `main` so the Phase 1A\r\n  hardening commit is live before real-doc E2E.\r\n- Deploy `scan-session-notes` and `_shared/sessionIngestCore.ts` when Joshua chooses to\r\n  start Phase 1B.\r\n- Any model/secret configuration change.\r\n\r\n**Still future direct-build work:**\r\n- Phase 2 synthesis + review + dossier tabs.\r\n- Phase 3 `publish-session-bundle`, AI suggestions, and later cron.\r\n\r\n**Lovable/Supabase-dependent (narrow):**\r\n- Confirm Gmail/Drive/Calendar connector **scopes** cover reading Gemini Notes emails\r\n  and exporting the linked Doc (likely already true).\r\n- The later **cron** trigger for automatic scanning.\r\n- Secret presence: `LOVABLE_API_KEY`, Google connector keys.\r\n\r\nSee `lovable-handoff-prompt.md` and `~/Downloads/PHASE-1B-LOVABLE-PROMPT.md` for the\r\ncurrent deploy ask.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 11. Verification per phase\r\n\r\nEach phase ends with a manual checklist Joshua runs in the deployed app (needs his\r\nadmin login + real data), and every checklist includes explicit error-path tests:\r\n\r\n- Email with no Doc link.\r\n- Ambiguous / name-only match → must land in resolution queue, not auto-attach.\r\n- Missing or non-corroborating calendar event.\r\n- Re-scan of an already-ingested email → no duplicate (idempotency).\r\n- Partial approve → publish only approved parts; held parts stay invisible.\r\n- Regression: existing AI TA draft flow (`useAITADrafts`) and the current student\r\n  detail page still work.\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## 12. Open decisions\r\n\r\n**Resolved (no longer blocking Phase 1A):**\r\n- ~~**`class_id` vs `auth.users.id`**~~ → RESOLVED by inspection: `class_id` *is* the auth\r\n  user id (UUID as text). Use the safe `resolveStudentAuthId` helper; fail closed on\r\n  orphans. (§3b)\r\n- ~~**Transcript size bound**~~ → DECIDED: bucket is canonical/always; `content_text` only\r\n  when < ~100 KB. ## assistant

I found the core issue: the pieces exist, but they are separate automations. The right design is one event-driven student lifecycle:

`consultation completed → follow-up → payment → access → first booking → onboarding → student folder → first drill → session recap`

Verified current state:

- Payment already grants paid students platform and drill access when their account exists.
- LSAT U already emails you when onboarding is completed.
- Single-session booking and package booking are separate but already supported.
- Session transcript ingestion is only partly automated. Synthesis and publishing are not finished.
- Your inbox shows repeated manual friction: students clarify payment, book complimentary sessions by mistake, send 7Sage links manually, and wait for onboarding instructions.
- I found no automatic student-folder creation.
- The next-day consultation email is currently called a “pre-consultation check-in” in one workflow, but it is actually a post-consultation follow-up. Those should remain separate.
- Your saved workflow says 3:00 PM the next day, while your new request says around midday.

❓ **Q1** - **Which lifecycle categories should the first version support?**

My recommendation is:

1. Consultation  
2. Paid package student  
3. Paid single-session student  
4. Existing student session  
5. Exceptional cases such as complimentary bookings, refunds, or unmatched payments

The system should classify from booking type and payment data, not guess from transcript language.

➡️ Confirm this set, or name anything missing.

---

❓ **Q2** - **What should the consultation follow-up actually be?**

The message the day after the consultation should be a “post-consultation follow-up,” not a pre-consultation check-in.

My recommendation:

- Keep the pre-consultation reminder separate.
- Send the post-consultation follow-up at 12:00 PM America/Toronto the next day.
- Auto-send the standard version.
- Route uncertain cases to you when the transcript is missing, the offer is unclear, or the student already paid.

➡️ Should this be automatic by default, or should it create a draft for your approval?

---

❓ **Q3** - **What event should start the student onboarding workflow?**

My recommendation:

- Payment grants access immediately.
- The full onboarding workflow starts when all three exist:
  - confirmed payment
  - student account
  - first session booked
- If someone pays before creating an account, hold the onboarding state and match it when they sign up.
- For packages, payment can grant access before the first booking.
- For a single session, payment and booking should resolve together.

➡️ Is that the correct definition of “ready to onboard”?

---

❓ **Q4** - **When should drill access become available?**

The current app can grant drill access automatically after payment. I recommend:

- Enable drill access immediately after confirmed payment.
- Do not assign a personalized first drill until onboarding intake is complete.
- Use the intake answers and first-session context to choose the first drill.

That gives the student something useful to do without pretending the system knows their needs before intake.

➡️ Do you want drill access immediately after payment, or only after onboarding is completed?

---

❓ **Q5** - **How autonomous should post-session recaps be?**

The current session pipeline is still manual at the scan stage, and recap synthesis plus publishing are not complete.

My recommendation for the first reliable version:

- Automatically detect the Gemini notes.
- Match them to the student using email, name, and calendar evidence.
- Classify the session.
- Generate the recap, homework, drill recommendation, and next-session agenda.
- Put the result in your review queue.
- Publish only after your approval.

Later, standard low-risk recaps can auto-publish.

➡️ Should the first version require your approval before anything reaches a student?

---

❓ **Q6** - **When should the student folder be created?**

My recommendation is to create the folder as soon as payment, account identity, or a booked paid session establishes a real student record. Onboarding then fills the folder with the student’s intake details.

That prevents a first-session transcript from arriving before the folder exists. The app, Gmail, Stripe, and Calendar remain authoritative for operational facts. The student folder becomes your working record for notes, judgments, transcripts, and follow-up.

➡️ Is it acceptable to create the folder before the student completes onboarding?

---

❓ **Q7** - **What should the email to you contain?**

The current onboarding email is useful, but it is a standalone data dump. I recommend replacing it with one concise operational notification containing:

- student name and email
- package or single-session status
- first booking date
- onboarding answers
- drill-access status
- student-folder link
- the one next action, if any
- exceptions such as unmatched payment or missing account

➡️ Should this become your single “new student ready” notification?

---

❓ **Q8** - **How should odd single-session cases work?**

Your inbox shows the exact failure mode you described: a student booked what looked like a complimentary session, then asked to pay for it afterward and purchase a package later.

My recommendation:

- Treat single-session booking as its own product flow.
- Embed the $45 payment step into that flow.
- After payment, automatically grant drill access and create the student record.
- Handle complimentary bookings as an exception queue, not as a normal onboarding path.
- Never send a package-specific email to a single-session student unless they choose to upgrade.

➡️ Should the system charge and onboard single-session students automatically at booking, with complimentary or ambiguous bookings routed to you?

## My request:
&#x20;Yeah, so this post-consultation follow-up is separate from the pre-consultation check-in. The pre-consultation check-in should be sent 30 minutes before the session. The post-consultation follow-up should be sent midday the day after.

- Thank you. Thank you. Thank you. Okay. The other thing is payments should grant access immediately. So, when they enroll, the home page should allow them to pay, whether it's a single initial payment or a package. If they pay, is there a way to automatically grant them access? The platform is good, but automatic access should only be for those with a package. Those who don't have a package shouldn't have full access to the platform. If they pay for one hour, they should have access to the drill I assigned them. Thank you. And they should be able to see on their to-do list on their homepage if they have five-hour sessions or ten-hour sessions. If they have five hours, they should have five credits to schedule as they wish. If they have ten hours, they should be able to schedule ten sessions. For single sessions, they should be clearly indicated. I want this process to be hands-off. The drill access should be confirmed by me on my homepage as the admin, pressing a checkmark or an X to confirm or deny the request. I should be able to see all of the requests on my admin page for LSAT U and confirm them. The access should be given to them after the payment, not after onboarding. Onboarding should be considered complete once the payment is made.


## assistant

That clarifies the main policy. I’m updating the design around two distinct states: commercial onboarding happens at payment, while the longer intake is a separate student-profile step. I’m also checking the current access flags and admin controls because the code currently grants the full paid feature set to any payment, including a single session, which does not match what you want.

[]).find(\r\n    (u: { email?: string | null }) => (u.email ?? "").toLowerCase() === target,\r\n  );\r\n  // Someone can pay before they ever make an account. That is not an error:\r\n  // Joshua grants them by hand once they sign up, and the payment record still\r\n  // exists either way.\r\n  if (!user) return { granted: false, reason: "no_account" };\r\n\r\n  const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n  for (const flag of PAID_STUDENT_FLAGS) updates[flag] = true;\r\n\r\n  const { error } = await admin.from("profiles").update(updates).eq("class_id", user.id);\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return { granted: true, class_id: user.id };\r\n}\r\n\r\n\n\n===== 2 =====\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \'react\';\r\nimport { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate, useOutletContext, useParams } from \'react-router-dom\';\r\nimport { ArrowLeft, Activity, Plus } from \'lucide-react\';\r\nimport { format } from \'date-fns\';\r\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\r\nimport { useSelectedHubStudent, useStudentEmail, type HubStudentRow } from \'@/hooks/useHubStudents\';\r\nimport { formatLastActiveLabel, isUserOnline } from \'@/lib/presence\';\r\nimport AssignmentBuilder from \'@/components/teacher/AssignmentBuilder\';\r\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The student dossier (spec T3.2): everything about one student behind one\r\n * URL, with the five surfaces as real child routes so each is linkable and\r\n * back/forward work. Replaces the split between TeacherStudentDetail, the\r\n * StudentHub page, and the roster\'s inline expander.\r\n */\r\n\r\nexport interface DossierContext {\r\n  studentId: string;\r\n  student: HubStudentRow | undefined;\r\n  studentName: string | null;\r\n  studentEmail: string | null;\r\n  /** Opens the shared assign flow already scoped to this student. */\r\n  openAssign: () => void;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function useDossier() {\r\n  return useOutletContext<DossierContext>();\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst TABS = [\r\n  { to: \'overview\', label: \'Overview\' },\r\n  { to: \'work\', label: \'Work\' },\r\n  { to: \'notes\', label: \'Notes\' },\r\n  { to: \'messages\', label: \'Messages\' },\r\n  { to: \'schedule\', label: \'Schedule\' },\r\n] as const;\r\n\r\nexport default function StudentDossier() {\r\n  const { studentId } = useParams<{ studentId: string }>();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n\r\n  const student = useSelectedHubStudent(studentId ?? null);\r\n  // The roster query may not have resolved on a deep link, so fall back to the\r\n  // narrow per-student RPC rather than showing a nameless header.\r\n  const { data: rpcEmail } = useStudentEmail(studentId ?? null);\r\n\r\n  const [showAssign, setShowAssign] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const studentName = student?.display_name?.trim() || null;\r\n  const studentEmail = student?.email ?? rpcEmail ?? null;\r\n\r\n  const isOnline = isUserOnline(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\r\n  const lastActiveAbsolute = student?.last_seen_at\r\n    ? format(new Date(student.last_seen_at), \'MMM d, h:mm a\')\r\n    : null;\r\n\r\n  const context = useMemo<DossierContext>(\r\n    () => ({\r\n      studentId: studentId as string,\r\n      student,\r\n      studentName,\r\n      studentEmail,\r\n      openAssign: () => setShowAssign(true),\r\n    }),\r\n    [student, studentEmail, studentId, studentName],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  if (!studentId) return null;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <section className="flex h-full w-full flex-col" aria-label="Student dossier">\r\n      <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 pb-0 pt-4">\r\n        <button\r\n          type="button"\r\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/students\')}\r\n          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-black/45 transition hover:text-black"\r\n        >\r\n          <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n          Roster\r\n        </button>\r\n\r\n        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">\r\n          <div className="min-w-0">\r\n            <h1 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-tight text-black">\r\n              {studentName || \'Student\'}\r\n            </h1>\r\n            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45">\r\n              {studentEmail && <span className="truncate font-mono">{studentEmail}</span>}\r\n              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">\r\n                <Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\r\n                {isOnline ? \'Online on LSAT U\' : \'Offline\'}\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>\r\n              {lastActiveAbsolute && <span>· {lastActiveAbsolute}</span>}\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          <button\r\n            type="button"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowAssign(true)}\r\n            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover"\r\n          >\r\n            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2} />\r\n            Assign work\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        {/* Sub-tabs sit below the shell\'s room rail, so they take the ramp\'s\r\n            13px label step — subordinate to the rail\'s 14px without inventing\r\n            an undocumented size. */}\r\n        <nav className="-mb-px mt-3 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto" aria-label="Dossier sections">\r\n          {TABS.map((tab) => (\r\n            <NavLink\r\n              key={tab.to}\r\n              to={tab.to}\r\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\r\n                cn(\r\n                  \'whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition\',\r\n                  isActive\r\n                    ? \'border-black font-semibold text-black\'\r\n                    : \'border-transparent font-medium text-black/45 hover:text-black/75\',\r\n                )\r\n              }\r\n            >\r\n              {tab.label}\r\n            </NavLink>\r\n          ))}\r\n        </nav>\r\n      </header>\r\n\r\n      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">\r\n        <Outlet context={context} />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {showAssign && user && (\r\n        <AssignmentBuilder\r\n          studentId={studentId}\r\n          adminUserId={user.id}\r\n          onClose={() => setShowAssign(false)}\r\n        />\r\n      )}\r\n    </section>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n\n\n===== 3 =====\nGet-Content: \r\nLine |\r\n   2 |  Get-Content -Raw \'projects/lsatu/src/pages/teacher/sections/StudentsS …\r\n     |  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\r\n     | Cannot find path \'C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\src\\pages\\teacher\\sections\\StudentsSection.tsx\' because it does not exist.\r\n'}]

## assistant

Good. The policy is now clearer:

- Pre-consultation check-in: 30 minutes before the consultation.
- Post-consultation follow-up: midday the next day.
- Payment activates the student immediately.
- Packages receive full platform access and reusable booking credits.
- Single-session students receive only the drill access you approve or assign.
- Payment, not the intake form, marks commercial onboarding complete.

One important implementation fact: the current code grants the full paid feature set to every payment, including single sessions. That needs to change. The existing booking system already tracks package credits, so the main work is separating package access from single-session drill access and giving you an approval queue.

❓ **Q1** - **What should “onboarding complete” mean in the system?**

I recommend splitting the current single status into two:

- **Activated:** payment confirmed. Access and the admin notification happen immediately.
- **Intake complete:** the student finishes the profile questions. This enriches their student folder but does not block access, booking, or the first session.

The current LSAT U intake card would become a persistent “Complete your profile” task rather than the gate for access.

➡️ Should students be allowed to attend their first session and use an assigned drill without completing the intake?

---

❓ **Q2** - **What exactly should your checkmark or X approve?**

There are two possible meanings:

1. Approve the student’s general drill access.
2. Approve a specific drill assignment for that student.

My recommendation is the second:

- Payment creates a pending drill-access request.
- You see the student, payment type, first session, and proposed drill.
- Checkmark approves the specific drill.
- X denies or holds it with a reason.
- The student cannot open that drill until approved.
- Package students do not need this approval because their full access is already active.

➡️ Is the checkmark meant to approve the specific drill, or the student’s general ability to use drills?

---

❓ **Q3** - **What should a single-session student see after payment?**

My recommendation:

- A clear “Single session” status.
- Their booked session date.
- A task such as “Complete your assigned drill.”
- No package credits.
- No unrestricted bootcamps, practice tests, journals, or broad classroom materials.
- Access only to the drill or materials you explicitly assign.

➡️ Should single-session students also see the normal classroom shell and inbox, or only the assigned drill plus session details?

---

❓ **Q4** - **What should package access include?**

The current paid-access bundle includes classroom, drills, bootcamps, practice, journal, WAJ, chat, scheduling, and activity access. The booking system already tracks package credits such as 5 or 10.

I recommend the homepage show:

- Package name
- Credits purchased
- Credits used
- Credits remaining
- Sessions booked
- A direct “Schedule a session” action
- A visible onboarding/profile task if intake is incomplete

➡️ Should every package include all of those platform areas, or should some features remain individually controlled?

---

❓ **Q5** - **When should you receive the admin email?**

Because you now define onboarding as payment, I recommend two separate notifications:

1. **Immediate activation email:** payment, product type, account match, access granted, credits, and first booking status.
2. **Later intake email:** profile answers and student-folder information when the student completes the intake.

The first email tells you whether the student is ready to teach. The second gives you teaching context.

➡️ Do you want both emails, or one consolidated email that updates when intake is later completed?

---

❓ **Q6** - **Should the intake be required before the first drill is approved?**

My recommendation is:

- Payment grants the commercial access state immediately.
- You can approve a drill immediately if you already know what to assign.
- The intake remains a recommended task and helps AI suggest better drills.
- If the intake is incomplete, the admin page clearly says “Profile incomplete,” but it does not block you.

➡️ Should missing intake ever block drill approval or the first session?

---

❓ **Q7** - **Where should package purchases begin?**

I recommend two entry points that use the same backend:

- Consultation follow-up email links directly to the selected payment option.
- The authenticated homepage shows the same package and single-session choices for students who are already considering enrollment.

After payment, the system identifies the product from the payment link. You should never need to manually tell LSAT U whether it was a single session, 5-hour package, or 10-hour package.

➡️ Should the homepage show all purchase options, or should package choices remain available only through consultation follow-up links?

Primary\nrecord: never regenerate over an existing one; a redo gets a dated correction section.\n\n```markdown\n---\ntitle: Recap · <Student> · YYYY-MM-DD\ntype: recap\nrecord_kind: primary\nstudent: <slug>\nsession_date: YYYY-MM-DD\nsource: transcript-downloads/YYYY-MM-DD-<slug>-notes-by-gemini.md\nstatus: draft\nposted: false\nposted_at:\n---\n\n# <Concept the student needs>\n\n<Open directly with the governing distinction or decision. I have the intake definition now:

- Intake means the LSAT U questions.
- Intake happens first.
- Payment is required before the student attends.
- Intake and payment confirmation both go into the student folder.
- After both are complete, you can approve general access with a checkmark.
- Single-session students get inbox, classroom, and scheduling, but no materials or drills.
- The homepage should emphasize scheduling, not package details or credit balances.
- The CRM-style progress tracker is deferred.

❓ **Q1** - **When should scheduling become available?**

My recommendation is:

- Intake incomplete: no session attendance.
- Intake complete, unpaid: show payment instructions only.
- Paid, awaiting your approval: allow scheduling but keep materials locked.
- Approved: apply the correct access level.
- Single session: inbox, classroom shell, and scheduling only.
- Package: full platform access.

➡️ Should a paid student be able to schedule before you press the approval checkmark?

---

❓ **Q2** - **Should single-session students ever receive a drill?**

Your latest instruction says single-session students should have no drilling or other materials. That supersedes the earlier idea of giving them an assigned drill.

My recommendation is:

- Single-session: no drill access by default.
- Package: drill access after your approval.
- Any exception can be granted manually from the admin page.

➡️ Is that the final rule?

---

❓ **Q3** - **What does your approval checkmark control?**

I recommend one approval action that applies the access policy based on the paid product:

- Package payment + intake complete → checkmark grants full access.
- Single-session payment + intake complete → checkmark grants inbox, classroom shell, and scheduling only.
- X leaves the student pending or denied and records a reason.
- Payment status remains separate from access approval.

➡️ Should the checkmark work this way, with the product type determining the access level automatically?

---

❓ **Q4** - **When should you receive the admin notification?**

My recommendation is to send it only when both conditions are true:

1. The student completed the intake.
2. Payment was confirmed.

The email would say: “Access request ready for approval,” with the student-folder link and the payment confirmation attached or linked. Your admin page would show the same request, so the email is only an alert.

➡️ Should the notification happen at that point, before you approve access?

---

❓ **Q5** - **What should the student see on the homepage?**

I understand your preference as:

- A prominent “Schedule your session” action at the top.
- No package name.
- No credit count.
- No sales or payment dashboard after payment.
- Access to inbox and classroom according to your approval.
- Materials and drills hidden unless the access policy allows them.

The admin page would still show product type, payment status, credits, and access state for your operational use.

➡️ Is that the correct split between the student view and the admin view?

---

❓ **Q6** - **What should happen automatically after each completed session?**

The session-recap skill is already designed to:

- read the full transcript;
- write a concept-first recap;
- file it in the student folder;
- record assigned work and follow-ups;
- leave the student-facing post as a draft unless you explicitly approve publishing.

My recommendation is to automate transcript detection and recap drafting first, while keeping student publication behind your approval.

➡️ Should the recap be automatically filed as a draft for every tutoring session, with consultations excluded unless they later become students?

That resolves most of the workflow.

The model I now understand is:

1. Student completes LSAT U intake.
2. Student pays.
3. LSAT U shows you an in-app request and sends an email.
4. You verify payment and press the green approval button.
5. The request disappears from the pending queue.
6. The student can schedule and access the platform.
7. Package students receive broad access.
8. Single-session students receive only the questions, drills, or materials you assign. They do not receive unlimited library access.
9. Their intake answers are immediately visible on the admin student page and copied into their folder.

The important distinction is that “full platform access” means access to the platform and assigned work, not unlimited access to every resource for a single-session student.

❓ **Q1** - **Should the green button grant assignment-based access for single-session students?**

My recommendation:

- Package student: green button grants full package access.
- Single-session student: green button grants the platform shell, inbox, classroom, and scheduling, plus only the specific assignments you give them.
- Unassigned materials remain inaccessible.
- You can add or remove assigned access from the admin page.

➡️ Is that the final access model?

---

❓ **Q2** - **Should the approved request disappear but remain in history?**

I recommend removing it from the pending queue after approval while preserving an audit record showing:

- intake completed
- payment confirmed
- access approved
- who approved it
- when approval happened
- what access level was granted

➡️ Is that what you mean by “cleared from the page”?

---

❓ **Q3** - **Should every paid tutoring session automatically create a recap draft?**

The existing session-recap skill is designed to read the transcript, write the recap, file it in the student folder, and leave publication for your approval.

My recommendation:

- Tutoring session: automatic recap draft and folder filing.
- Consultation: no teaching recap unless the person later becomes a student.
- Missing transcript: show an exception on your admin page.
- Student-facing publication: still requires your approval.

## assistant

Good. The workflow is now coherent.

The system should have two queues on your LSAT U admin page:

1. Activation queue

When intake is completed and payment is confirmed:

- You receive an in-app notification and email.
- You see the student’s intake answers and payment status immediately.
- Green checkmark: activate the student.
- Cancel: dismiss the request without rejecting the student.
- Activation updates the student folder with intake and payment details.
- The pending request disappears, but approval history remains.

Access rules:

- Package student: full package access.
- Single-session student: inbox, classroom shell, scheduling, and only the questions or materials you assign.
- Scheduling stays prominent on the student homepage.
- No unrestricted library access for single-session students.

2. Post-session recap queue

After every paid tutoring session:

- Gemini transcript is detected.
- A recap draft is generated and filed in the student folder.
- You receive an in-app notification.
- Clicking it opens a properly formatted preview.
- You can edit and approve it.
- Approval automatically publishes the recap and assigns the supported homework or drill items.

Consultations remain separate:

- Check-in email 30 minutes before.
- Post-consultation follow-up at midday the next day.
- No tutoring recap unless the prospect becomes a student.

The key implementation is one event ledger connecting payment, intake, activation, folders, transcripts, recap drafts, approvals, and assignments. That will make the process hands-off without giving single-session students unlimited access.

No files or product behavior have been changed yet.

If a step is impossible as written, implement the\n  closest faithful version and report the deviation — do not redesign.\nKEY PATHS: <files/dirs Codex will touch or must read first>\nCONSTRAINTS: <"don\'t touch X", style rules, deps that must not change>\nNON-GOALS: <explicitly out of scope — from the plan\'s Out of scope section>\nPROOF: Run `<PROOF_CMD>` and include its full output in your report.\nOUTPUT: End with a report — files changed (one line each: path + what/why),\n  proof output, and any deviations from the spec with reasons.\nEOF\n```\n\n## Step 2 — Launch Codex (fresh session, capture `thread_id`)\n\n```bash\ncodex exec --yolo --json -o /tmp/codex-build.txt - <"$P" 2>/dev/null | grep \'"type":"thread.started"\'\n```\n\n- Prompt goes via stdin (`- <"$P"`) — this both avoids quoting bugs AND sidesteps the non-TTY stdin hang (`codex exec` blocks forever waiting on stdin EOF under Codex\'s Bash tool; feeding the file gives immediate EOF).\n- Parse `thread_id` from the `{"type":"thread.started","thread_id":"..."}` line → `THREAD_ID`. Codex\'s final report lands in `/tmp/codex-build.txt` — read that file; don\'t parse the JSONL stream for content.\n- `2>/dev/null` suppresses cosmetic MCP/auth stderr noise. Confirm success by the report file + a `thread.started` line; neither → failed run (auth/model) — stop and tell the user.\n- **Timing:** foreground with `timeout: 600000` on the Bash tool call (default 2-min tool timeout kills real builds). If the spec is clearly >10 min of work (multi-file feature, migration, anything with image generation), launch with `run_in_background: true` instead and read the `-o` file when it exits. Don\'t kill a quiet background run early — Codex builds are legitimately slow.\n- **Heads-up on completion (required):** when a background Codex run finishes, the FIRST line of your next message to the user must be a loud standalone banner — `🔔 CODEX FINISHED — <what> (exit ok/fail) — verifying now` — BEFORE any verification output. The user is not watching tool calls; never let a completed build slide silently into the verify phase.\n\n## Step 3 — Verify (Codex, always, never delegated)\n\nCodex\'s report is advisory. Verify yourself:\n\n1. `git status -sb` + read the FULL diff (`git diff`). Judge it like a contributor PR: correctness, spec fidelity, style match with surrounding code, nothing touched outside scope.\n2. Run `PROOF_CMD` yourself (or the focused tests for the changed area). Codex\'s pasted output doesn\'t count as proof.\n3. Append to `LOG_FILE` under `## Act 3 — Build`: `### Round <n> — Codex build` + its report summary + `### Codex\'s verdict` + what passed/failed review.\n\n## Step 4 — Fix loop (same session, bounded)\n\nProblems found → resume the SAME session (Codex keeps its context; cheaper and better than a fresh run). Write the fix list to a temp file (`$P2`), same contract discipline: exact problem, exact file, proof expected.\n\n```bash\n# resume has no --yolo and no -C: run from the repo dir and spell the long flag,\n# or Codex inherits config.toml\'s sandbox (possibly read-only) and can\'t write.\ncodex exec resume "$THREAD_ID" --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox --json \\\n  -o /tmp/codex-build.txt - <"$P2" 2>/dev/null >/dev/null\n```\n\nRe-verify (Step 3) after each round. After `MAX_FIX_ROUNDS` failed rounds: STOP delegating — Codex takes over and finishes the remaining fixes directly. Log the takeover. Ping-ponging trivia through delegation burns more than it saves.\n\n## Step 5 — Human gate (diff sign-off)\n\nPresent: 3-bullet summary of what was built, files-changed list, proof-test output (pass/fail, verbatim tail), rounds used, any spec deviations. Ask: *"Codex built it, proof passes, diff reviewed. Commit?"*\n\n- Commit ONLY on yes — and Codex writes the commit, never Codex.\n- Rejected → ask what\'s wrong, route back to Step 4 (or take over directly if fix rounds are spent).\n\n## Hard rules\n\n- Clean tree before launch. Always. No exceptions.\n- Codex never skips the diff read. Codex claims are advisory until Codex has read the diff and run the proof.\n- Fix loop terminates at `MAX_FIX_ROUNDS` — then Codex takes over. No unbounded delegation ping-pong.\n- Commits, pushes, releases, GitHub mutations: Codex-side only, after the human gate. Codex never commits.\n- `LOG_FILE` is the deliverable — with Acts 1/2 it tells the whole story: grilled → reviewed → built → verified.\n\n## What NOT to do\n\n- Don\'t build without a spec — that\'s designing by delegation, and it fails. Route to `/grill-me-codex` or `/codex-review` first.\n- Don\'t use for ~<20-line single-obvious-change edits — just make the edit.\n- Don\'t pin `-codex` model variants on ChatGPT-account auth — 400s.\n- Don\'t resume with `--last` — capture and use the explicit `THREAD_ID` (parallel sessions make `--last` grab the wrong thread). And ECHO the id into the command visibly before running: `resume` with a missing/garbage id can silently fall back to the most recent session instead of erroring (observed 2026-07-08) — a wrong-target resume looks exactly like a successful one.\n- Don\'t parse the JSONL stream for the report — read the `-o` file.\n- Don\'t let Codex commit, and don\'t auto-commit yourself — human gate first.\n\r\n'}]
