useInbox();\n\n  const rows = useMemo<HubStudentRow[]>(() => {\n    const users = usersQuery.data ?? [];\n    const counts = countsQuery.data ?? [];\n\n    const countsByStudent = new Map<string, AssignmentCountsRow>();\n    for (const c of counts) countsByStudent.set(c.student_id, c);\n\n    // Build a set of student user_ids that have unread messages waiting\n    // for the admin. A conversation with unread=true means the last\n    // message is from the non-admin participant — so the other\n    // participant is the student.\n    const unreadStudents = new Set<string>();\n    for (const c of conversations) {\n      if (!c.unread) continue;\n      for (const p of c.participants) {\n        if (!p.is_admin) unreadStudents.add(p.user_id);\n      }\n    }\n\n    return users\n      // The admin-manage-users payload includes admins themselves; hide\n      // them from the Student Hub list.\n      .filter((u) => u.role !== "admin")\n      .map((u) => {\n        const c = countsByStudent.get(u.class_id);\n        return {\n          ...u,\n          assignments_total: c?.total ?? 0,\n          assignments_assigned: c?.assigned ?? 0,\n          assignments_viewed: c?.viewed ?? 0,\n          assignments_completed: c?.completed ?? 0,\n          has_unread_from_student: unreadStudents.has(u.class_id),\n        };\n      });\n  }, [usersQuery.data, countsQuery.data, conversations]);\n\n  return {\n    rows,\n    // Loading is true only while the *core* roster is loading. Counts and\n    // unread-pip queries are decorative — the list still works without them.\n    loading: usersQuery.isLoading,\n    // Error is reported only when the core roster fails. Count/inbox failures\n    // log a warning but the list still renders with zeros / no pips so\n    // assignment workflows aren\'t blocked by a peripheral hiccup.\n    error: usersQuery.error ?? null,\n    refresh: () => {\n      usersQuery.refetch();\n      countsQuery.refetch();\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Memoized single-row lookup. Stable object reference as long as `rows`\n * and `studentId` are stable — avoids churn in downstream consumers.\n */\nexport function useSelectedHubStudent(\n  studentId: string | null\n): HubStudentRow | undefined {\n  const { rows } = useHubStudents();\n  return useMemo(() => {\n    if (!studentId) return undefined;\n    return rows.find((r) => r.class_id === studentId);\n  }, [rows, studentId]);\n}\n\n/**\n * Narrow per-student email lookup via the `get_student_email` RPC.\n * Use this when the caller hasn\'t already loaded the full hub roster\n * (e.g. deep-linked Overview before the list query resolves). Falls\n * back to NULL if the caller is not an admin (RPC body enforces).\n */\nexport function useStudentEmail(studentId: string | null) {\n  return useQuery<string | null>({\n    queryKey: ["hub-student-email", studentId],\n    enabled: !!studentId,\n    staleTime: HUB_STALE_MS,\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      if (!studentId) return null;\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(\n        "get_student_email",\n        { _student_id: studentId }\n      );\n      if (error) throw error;\n      return data ?? null;\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Legacy helper retained for any callers that already have `rows` in\n * scope. Prefer `useSelectedHubStudent` in new code.\n */\nexport function selectHubStudent(\n  rows: HubStudentRow[],\n  studentId: string | null\n): HubStudentRow | undefined {\n  if (!studentId) return undefined;\n  return rows.find((r) => r.class_id === studentId);\n}\n\r\n\n\n===== 3 =====\nimport { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",\n};\n\n// The primary admin account can never be demoted, so the system always retains\n// at least one admin and can never be locked out of role management.\nconst PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL = "contact@aspiringattorneys.com";\n\nconst ALL_FLAGS = [\n  "has_bootcamp_access",\n  "has_classroom_access",\n  "has_analytics_access",\n  "has_schedule_access",\n  "has_practice_access",\n  "has_drill_access",\n  "has_waj_access",\n  "has_flagged_access",\n  "has_chat_access",\n  "has_export_access",\n  "has_ta_access",\n  "has_journal_access",\n  "has_activity_access",\n];\n\nasync function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!;\n  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;\n  const anonKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!;\n\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");\n  const token = authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length);\n\n  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey);\n  const { data: claimsData, error } = await anonClient.auth.getClaims(token);\n  if (error || !claimsData?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");\n  const user = { id: claimsData.claims.sub as string };\n\n  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);\n  const { data: roleData, error: roleLookupError } = await admin\n    .from("user_roles")\n    .select("role")\n    .eq("user_id", user.id)\n    .eq("role", "admin")\n    .maybeSingle();\n\n  if (roleLookupError) {\n    console.error("verifyAdmin: role lookup failed", {\n      user_id: user.id,\n      error: roleLookupError,\n    });\n    throw new Error("Forbidden");\n  }\n  if (!roleData) throw new Error("Forbidden");\n\n  return { admin, callerId: user.id };\n}\n\nfunction jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\n    status,\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n  });\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const { admin } = await verifyAdmin(req);\n\n    // ── GET: list users + analytics ──\n    if (req.method === "GET") {\n      const url = new URL(req.url);\n      const mode = url.searchParams.get("mode");\n\n      // Analytics mode\n      if (mode === "analytics") {\n        const { data: profiles } = await admin\n          .from("profiles")\n          .select("class_id, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\n\n        const { data: { users: authUsers } } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\n\n        const now = new Date();\n        const dayAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\n        const weekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\n\n        const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\n        const adminCount = (roles || []).filter((r: any) => r.role === "admin").length;\n\n        const activeToday = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > dayAgo\n        ).length;\n\n        const activeWeek = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) =>\n          p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at) > weekAgo\n        ).length;\n\n        // Feature adoption counts\n        const adoption: Record<string, number> = {};\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\n          adoption[flag] = (profiles || []).filter((p: any) => p[flag] === true).length;\n        }\n\n        // Recent attempts stats\n        const { count: totalAttempts } = await admin\n          .from("attempts")\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true });\n\n        const { count: weekAttempts } = await admin\n          .from("attempts")\n          .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })\n          .gte("timestamp_iso", weekAgo.toISOString());\n\n        return jsonRes({\n          total_users: authUsers?.length || 0,\n          active_today: activeToday,\n          active_week: activeWeek,\n          admin_count: adminCount,\n          feature_adoption: adoption,\n          total_attempts: totalAttempts || 0,\n          week_attempts: weekAttempts || 0,\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Default: list users\n      const { data: profiles, error: pErr } = await admin\n        .from("profiles")\n        .select("class_id, display_name, last_seen_at, " + ALL_FLAGS.join(", "));\n      if (pErr) throw pErr;\n\n      const { data: { users: authUsers }, error: aErr } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\n      if (aErr) throw aErr;\n\n      const emailMap = new Map(authUsers.map((u: any) => [u.id, u.email]));\n\n      const { data: roles } = await admin.from("user_roles").select("user_id, role");\n      const roleMap = new Map<string, string>();\n      for (const r of roles || []) {\n        roleMap.set(r.user_id, r.role);\n      }\n\n      const result = (profiles || []).map((p: any) => ({\n        ...p,\n        email: emailMap.get(p.class_id) || "unknown",\n        role: roleMap.get(p.class_id) || "user",\n      }));\n\n      return jsonRes(result);\n    }\n\n    // ── POST: toggle flag, bulk actions, role management ──\n    if (req.method === "POST") {\n      const body = await req.json();\n      const { action } = body;\n\n      // Toggle single flag\n      if (!action || action === "toggle") {\n        const { class_id, field, value } = body;\n        if (!class_id || !ALL_FLAGS.includes(field) || typeof value !== "boolean") {\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n        }\n        // Return the row we actually wrote. A filter that matches nothing is\n        // not an error in PostgREST, so without this the dashboard would show\n        // a successful toggle that never landed and then snap back on reload.\n        const { data: updated, error } = await admin\n          .from("profiles")\n          .update({ [field]: value })\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\n          .select("class_id, " + field);\n        if (error) throw error;\n        if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {\n          console.error("toggle: no profile matched", { class_id, field, value });\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\n        }\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: updated[0] });\n      }\n\n      // Apply a template (explicit flag map) to a user. One DB update so\n      // the user never sits in a partially-applied state.\n      if (action === "apply_template") {\n        const { class_id, flags } = body;\n        if (!class_id || !flags || typeof flags !== "object") {\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n        }\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) {\n          if (typeof flags[flag] === "boolean") {\n            updates[flag] = flags[flag];\n          }\n        }\n        if (Object.keys(updates).length === 0) {\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No valid flags" }, 400);\n        }\n        const { error } = await admin\n          .from("profiles")\n          .update(updates)\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\n        if (error) throw error;\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\n      }\n\n      // Bulk grant/revoke all flags for a user\n      if (action === "bulk_user") {\n        const { class_id, value } = body;\n        if (!class_id || typeof value !== "boolean") {\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n        }\n        const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\n        for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = value;\n        const { error } = await admin\n          .from("profiles")\n          .update(updates)\n          .eq("class_id", class_id);\n        if (error) throw error;\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\n      }\n\n      // Put an account back to the state a brand-new signup is in: no intake\n      // answers, no name, the intake email re-armed, and every paid door shut.\n      // Built for testing the signup path end to end without hand-written SQL,\n      // and for the case where a student needs to answer the intake again.\n      //\n      // Deliberately does not touch auth: the account, its password and its\n      // work all survive. Nothing here deletes a student\'s practice history.\n      if (action === "reset_onboarding") {\n        const { class_id, lock = true } = body;\n        if (!class_id) return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n\n        const updates: Record<string, unknown> = {\n          onboarding: null,\n          onboarding_completed_at: null,\n          onboarding_notified_at: null,\n          display_name: null,\n        };\n        if (lock) {\n          for (const flag of ALL_FLAGS) updates[flag] = false;\n          // The three a locked account still needs: somewhere to stand, a way\n          // to reach Joshua, and a way to book.\n          updates.has_classroom_access = true;\n          updates.has_chat_access = true;\n          updates.has_schedule_access = true;\n        }\n\n        const { data: reset, error } = await admin\n          .from("profiles")\n          .update(updates)\n          .eq("class_id", class_id)\n          .select("class_id, display_name, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at");\n        if (error) throw error;\n        if (!reset || reset.length === 0) {\n          console.error("reset_onboarding: no profile matched", { class_id });\n          return jsonRes({ error: "No profile matched that class_id" }, 404);\n        }\n        return jsonRes({ success: true, profile: reset[0] });\n      }\n\n      // Set role\n      if (action === "set_role") {\n        const { user_id, role } = body;\n        if (!user_id || !["admin", "user"].includes(role)) {\n          return jsonRes({ error: "Invalid params" }, 400);\n        }\n        // Protect the primary admin: it can never be demoted, by anyone\n        // (including itself). This guarantees the system keeps at least one\n        // admin and cannot be locked out.\n        if (role === "user") {\n          const { data: target } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(user_id);\n          if ((target?.user?.email || "").toLowerCase() === PRIMARY_ADMIN_EMAIL) {\n            return jsonRes({ error: "The primary admin account cannot be demoted." }, 403);\n          }\n        }\n        if (role === "admin") {\n          const { error: upsertError } = await admin\n            .from("user_roles")\n            .upsert({ user_id, role: "admin" }, { onConflict: "user_id,role" });\n          if (upsertError) {\n            console.error("set_role: upsert failed", { user_id, error: upsertError });\n            throw upsertError;\n          }\n        } else {\n          const { error: deleteError } = await admin\n            .from("user_roles")\n            .delete()\n            .eq("user_id", user_id)\n            .eq("role", "admin");\n          if (deleteError) {\n            console.error("set_role: delete failed", { user_id, error: deleteError });\n            throw deleteError;\n          }\n        }\n        return jsonRes({ success: true });\n      }\n\n      return jsonRes({ error: "Unknown action" }, 400);\n    }\n\n    return jsonRes({ error: "Method not allowed" }, 405);\n  } catch (err) {\n    const msg = (err as Error).message;\n    const status = msg === "Forbidden" ? 403 : msg === "Unauthorized" || msg === "Missing auth" ? 401 : 500;\n    return jsonRes({ error: msg }, status);\n  }\n});\n\r\n\n\n===== 4 =====\nimport { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";\nimport { sendAssignmentEmail } from "@/lib/assignmentEmail";\n\n/**\n * Notify a student that a new classroom_items row has been created for them.\n * Posts a message to the 1:1 admin↔student inbox thread (creating it if\n * needed), inserts a bell notifications row, and emails the student.\n *\n * Non-throwing. Returns granular flags so callers can surface partial-success\n * states truthfully ("Assigned, but email failed").\n *\n * Email is sent via the `send-assignment-email` edge function, which uses\n * Resend. If RESEND_API_KEY or FROM_EMAIL is unset the function returns\n * a truthful failure — we never fake email_ok.\n */\nexport interface NotifyResult {\n  inbox_ok: boolean;\n  bell_ok: boolean;\n  email_ok: boolean;\n  inbox_error?: string;\n  bell_error?: string;\n  email_error?: string;\n}\n\ninterface NotifyParams {\n  adminUserId: string;\n  studentId: string;\n  title: string;\n  /** Path the student should land on, e.g. /classroom/item/<id> */\n  route: string;\n  /** Short body shown in the inbox message + bell notification (optional). */\n  description?: string;\n  /** Type label used in the email body, e.g. "homework", "bootcamp_module". */\n  assignmentType?: string;\n}\n\nasync function findOrCreateThread(adminUserId: string, studentId: string): Promise<string> {\n  const { data: myParts, error: myPartsErr } = await supabase\n    .from("conversation_participants")\n    .select("conversation_id")\n    .eq("user_id", adminUserId);\n  if (myPartsErr) throw myPartsErr;\n\n  const myConvIds = (myParts ?? []).map((p) => p.conversation_id);\n  if (myConvIds.length > 0) {\n    const { data: shared, error: sharedErr } = await supabase\n      .from("conversation_participants")\n      .select("conversation_id")\n      .eq("user_id", studentId)\n      .in("conversation_id", myConvIds)\n      .limit(1);\n    if (sharedErr) throw sharedErr;\n    if (shared && shared.length > 0) return shared[0].conversation_id;\n  }\n\n  const { data: conv, error: cErr } = await supabase\n    .from("conversations")\n    .insert({ subject: null, created_by: adminUserId })\n    .select()\n    .single();\n  if (cErr || !conv) throw cErr ?? new Error("Failed to create conversation");\n\n  const { error: pErr } = await supabase\n    .from("conversation_participants")\n    .insert([\n      { conversation_id: conv.id, user_id: adminUserId },\n      { conversation_id: conv.id, user_id: studentId },\n    ]);\n  if (pErr) throw pErr;\n\n  return conv.id;\n}\n\nexport async function notifyStudentOfClassroomItem(\n  params: NotifyParams,\n): Promise<NotifyResult> {\n  const { adminUserId, studentId, title, route, description, assignmentType } = params;\n\n  const result: NotifyResult = {\n    inbox_ok: false,\n    bell_ok: false,\n    email_ok: false,\n  };\n\n  try {\n    const conversationId = await findOrCreateThread(adminUserId, studentId);\n    const body =\n      `New assignment: ${title}\\n\\n` +\n      (description ? `${description}\\n\\n` : "") +\n      `View in your Classroom: ${route}`;\n    const { error: mErr } = await supabase\n      .from("messages")\n      .insert({\n        conversation_id: conversationId,\n        sender_id: adminUserId,\n        body,\n      });\n    if (mErr) throw mErr;\n    result.inbox_ok = true;\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error("[classroomNotify:inbox] failed", { studentId, error: e });\n    result.inbox_error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "inbox failed";\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const { error: nErr } = await (supabase as unknown as {\n      from: (t: string) => {\n        insert: (v: unknown) => Promise<{ error: { message: string } | null }>;\n      };\n    })\n      .from("notifications")\n      .insert({\n        user_id: studentId,\n        type: "assignment",\n        title: `New assignment: ${title}`,\n        body: description ?? null,\n        link: route,\n      });\n    if (nErr) throw nErr;\n    result.bell_ok = true;\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error("[classroomNotify:bell] failed", { studentId, error: e });\n    result.bell_error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "bell failed";\n  }\n\n  // Email step. Failure is recorded but the assignment is not affected.\n  const emailRes = await sendAssignmentEmail({\n    studentId,\n    title,\n    description,\n    route,\n    assignmentType: assignmentType ?? "assignment",\n  });\n  result.email_ok = emailRes.ok;\n  if (!emailRes.ok) result.email_error = emailRes.error;\n\n  return result;\n}\n\n/**\n * One-line summary suitable for a toast.\n *   - "Assigned and notified"            (all three ok)\n *   - "Assigned, but email failed"        (inbox+bell ok, email failed)\n *   - "Assigned, partial notification"    (some other partial)\n *   - "Assigned, but notification failed" (all three failed)\n */\nexport function summarizeNotifyResult(r: NotifyResult): string {\n  if (r.inbox_ok && r.bell_ok && r.email_ok) return "Assigned and notified";\n  if (!r.inbox_ok && !r.bell_ok && !r.email_ok) return "Assigned, but notification failed";\n  if (r.inbox_ok && r.bell_ok && !r.email_ok) return "Assigned, but email failed";\n  return "Assigned, partial notification";\n}\n\r\n'}]

## Tool: exec

const cmds=[
"Get-Content -Raw 'supabase/functions/_shared/sendAppEmail.ts'",
"Get-Content -Raw 'supabase/functions/_shared/transactional-email-templates/onboarding-intake.tsx'",
"Get-Content -Raw 'src/pages/teacher/TeacherToday.tsx'",

"Get-Content -Raw 'src/pages/teacher/students/useStudentAccess.ts'"
];
const results=await Promise.all(cmds.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,workdir:"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\lsatu-lifecycle",yield_time_ms:10000,max_output_tokens:12000})));
for(let i=0;i<results.length;i++) text(`\n===== ${i} =====\n${results[i].output}`);


## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== 0 =====\nimport { createClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2'\nimport {\n  sendTemplateEmail,\n  type SendTemplateEmailResult,\n} from './transactional-email-templates/send-email.ts'\n\n/**\n * Shared app-email send path.\n *\n * Sends a registered template through Lovable's managed email API and keeps the\n * project's own `email_send_log` audit trail in sync:\n *   - `sent`       after a successful send\n *   - `suppressed` when the recipient is suppressed (bounce/complaint/unsubscribe)\n *   - `failed`     when the send throws\n *\n * Delivery, retries, rate limiting, suppression enforcement and the unsubscribe\n * footer are handled server-side, so there is nothing to enqueue here.\n */\nexport interface SendAppEmailOptions {\n  templateData?: Record<string, unknown>\n  idempotencyKey?: string\n  replyTo?: string\n}\n\nfunction logClient() {\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')\n  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')\n  if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceKey) return null\n  return createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey)\n}\n\nasync function writeLog(\n  templateName: string,\n  recipientEmail: string,\n  status: 'sent' | 'suppressed' | 'failed',\n  errorMessage?: string,\n) {\n  const supabase = logClient()\n  if (!supabase) return\n  const { error } = await supabase.from('email_send_log').insert({\n    message_id: null,\n    template_name: templateName,\n    recipient_email: recipientEmail,\n    status,\n    error_message: errorMessage ?? null,\n  })\n  if (error) {\n    console.error('email_send_log insert failed', { status, templateName, error })\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function sendAppEmail(\n  templateName: string,\n  recipientEmail: string,\n  options: SendAppEmailOptions = {},\n): Promise<SendTemplateEmailResult> {\n  let result: SendTemplateEmailResult\n  try {\n    result = await sendTemplateEmail(templateName, recipientEmail, {\n      templateData: options.templateData as Record<string, any> | undefined,\n      idempotencyKey: options.idempotencyKey,\n      replyTo: options.replyTo,\n    })\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)\n    await writeLog(templateName, recipientEmail, 'failed', message.slice(0, 1000))\n    throw error\n  }\n\n  if (result.sent) {\n    await writeLog(templateName, recipientEmail, 'sent')\n  } else {\n    await writeLog(templateName, recipientEmail, 'suppressed')\n  }\n  return result\n}\n\r\n"}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== 1 =====\nimport * as React from \'npm:react@18.3.1\'\nimport {\n  Body, Container, Head, Heading, Html, Preview, Text,\n} from \'npm:@react-email/components@0.0.22\'\nimport type { TemplateEntry } from \'./registry.ts\'\n\n// The intake notification goes to Joshua, never to the student. The whole\n// Markdown file sits in the body as preformatted text: it reads fine on a\n// phone, and it is the exact text that gets filed into the student\'s folder,\n// so there is no second rendering that can drift from the first.\n\ninterface OnboardingIntakeProps {\n  studentName?: string\n  studentEmail?: string\n  markdown?: string\n}\n\nconst OnboardingIntakeEmail = ({\n  studentName = \'A student\',\n  studentEmail = \'\',\n  markdown = \'\',\n}: OnboardingIntakeProps) => (\n  <Html lang="en" dir="ltr">\n    <Head />\n    <Preview>{`${studentName} finished onboarding`}</Preview>\n    <Body style={main}>\n      <Container style={container}>\n        <Heading style={h1}>{studentName} finished onboarding</Heading>\n        <Text style={text}>{studentEmail}</Text>\n        <pre style={pre}>{markdown}</pre>\n      </Container>\n    </Body>\n  </Html>\n)\n\nconst main = { backgroundColor: \'#ffffff\', fontFamily: \'ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif\' }\nconst container = { margin: \'0 auto\', padding: \'24px\', maxWidth: \'640px\' }\nconst h1 = { fontSize: \'20px\', fontWeight: 600, color: \'#111111\', margin: \'0 0 4px\' }\nconst text = { fontSize: \'13px\', color: \'#666666\', margin: \'0 0 16px\' }\nconst pre = {\n  fontFamily: \'ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace\',\n  fontSize: \'12px\',\n  lineHeight: \'1.6\',\n  color: \'#111111\',\n  whiteSpace: \'pre-wrap\' as const,\n  wordBreak: \'break-word\' as const,\n  border: \'1px solid #e5e5e5\',\n  padding: \'16px\',\n  margin: 0,\n}\n\nexport const template: TemplateEntry = {\n  component: OnboardingIntakeEmail,\n  // A fixed, greppable subject: the filing skill finds these by subject alone.\n  subject: (d: Record<string, any>) => `[LSATU ONBOARDING] ${d.studentName ?? \'Unknown student\'}`,\n  to: \'contact@aspiringattorneys.com\',\n  displayName: \'Onboarding intake (to Joshua)\',\n  previewData: {\n    studentName: \'Sofia Minassian\',\n    studentEmail: \'sofia@example.com\',\n    markdown: \'---\\nstudent: Sofia Minassian\\n---\\n\\n# Onboarding intake - Sofia Minassian\\n\',\n  },\n}\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== 2 =====\nimport { useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\nimport {\n  ClipboardList,\n  Sparkles,\n  UploadCloud,\n  MessagesSquare,\n  CalendarDays,\n} from \'lucide-react\';\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\nimport { useHubStudents } from \'@/hooks/useHubStudents\';\n\n// Counts are nullable on purpose: a query that failed has no count, and the\n// tiles must say so rather than print a zero the data never supported.\ninterface DashboardStats {\n  pendingDrafts: number | null;\n  uploadsToReview: number | null;\n}\n\nfunction useDashboardStats(): DashboardStats {\n  const { data } = useQuery<DashboardStats>({\n    queryKey: [\'teacher-today-stats\'],\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      const [draftsRes, uploadsRes] = await Promise.all([\n        supabase\n          .from(\'ai_ta_drafts\')\n          .select(\'*\', { count: \'exact\', head: true })\n          .eq(\'status\', \'draft\'),\n        supabase\n          .from(\'external_work_uploads\')\n          .select(\'*\', { count: \'exact\', head: true })\n default function TeacherToday() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const stats = useDashboardStats();\n  const { rows: students = [], error: rosterError } = useHubStudents();\n\n  // The roster students.length;\n\n  return (\n    <div className="px-6 py-8 max-w-5xl space-y-8">\n      <div className="space-y-1.5">\n        <div className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-500">\n          Teacher\n        </div>\n        <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Today</h2>\n        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">\n          Quick view of review"\n          value={stats.uploadsToReview}\n          Icon={UploadCloud}\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/uploads\')}\n        />\n        <StatTile\n          label="Students needing reply"\n          value={needsReplyCount}\n          Icon={MessagesSquare}\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/inbox\')}\n        />\n        <StatTile\n          label="Roster"\n          value={rosterCount}\n          Icon={ClipboardList}\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher?ws=people\')}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      <section className="space-y-3">\n        <h3 className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 font-semibold">\n          Quick actions\n        </h3>\n        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2">\n          <ActionRow\n            Icon={CalendarDays}\n            title="Build a weekly plan"\n            description="Drag drills and homework into a 7-day plan and publish it."\n            onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/weekly-plans\')}\n          />\n          <ActionRow\n            Icon={ClipboardList}\n            title="Assign homework or drills"\n            description="Pick a student and push assignable items into their classroom."\n            onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/assignments\')}\n          />\n        </div>\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction StatTile({\n  label,\n  value,\n  Icon,\n  onClick,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  value: number | null;\n  Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;\n  onClick: () => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <button\n      type="button"\n      onClick={onClick}\n      className="group text-left rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/50 hover:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors px-4 py-3.5"\n    >\n      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-500">\n        <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />\n        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider">{label}</span>\n      </div>\n      <div\n        className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-zinc-100 tabular-nums"\n        title={value === null ? "Couldn\'t load" : undefined}\n      >\n        {value === null ? \'—\' : value}\n      </div>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ActionRow({\n  Icon,\n  title,\n  description,\n  onClick,\n}: {\n  Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  onClick: () => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <button\n      type="button"\n      onClick={onClick}\n      className="text-left flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/40 hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-zinc-700 transition-colors px-4 py-3"\n    >\n      <Icon className="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />\n      <div className="min-w-0">\n        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100">{title}</div>\n        <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">{description}</div>\n      </div>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\n===== 3 =====\nimport { useState } from \'react\';\nimport { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\nimport {\n  BarChart3,\n  LayoutGrid,\n  Library,\n  MessageSquare,\n  PanelRightClose,\n  PanelRightOpen,\n  PencilRuler,\n  type LucideIcon,\n} from \'lucide-react\';\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\nimport { OperatorChatProvider } from \'@/components/teacher/ai-ta/OperatorChatProvider\';\n\ntype Room = {\n  to: string;\n  label: string;\n  Icon: LucideIcon;\n  /** NavLink `end`: only Today, so exactly one item is ever active. */\n  end?: boolean;\n};\n\n/**\n * The four rooms in the teacher workspace. Students live in the classroom\n * side rail because roster access is a teacher-level destination, not a room\n * beside the teacher\'s daily work. This list is the only top navigation in\n * the teacher workspace: no `?ws=` switching, no second rail,\n * no per-surface tab bars pretending to be nav.\n *\n * `end` is set on Today alone. Every other room owns a path prefix, so its\n * child routes (library tabs, a student dossier, a thread) keep the parent\n * highlighted, and no two items can be active at once.\n */\nconst ROOMS: Room[] = [\n  { to: \'/teacher/today\', label: \'Today\', Icon: LayoutGrid, end: true },\n  { to: \'/teacher/inbox\', label: \'Inbox\', Icon: MessageSquare },\n  { to: \'/teacher/library\', label: \'Library\', Icon: Library },\n  { to: \'/teacher/insights\', label: \'Insights\', Icon: BarChart3 },\n];\n\n/**\n * The perspective floor from the Desk. Kept so the workspace ground reads as\n * the same room the Desk surfaces were designed for.\n */\nconst FLOOR_GRID_STYLE: React.CSSProperties = {\n  transform: \'perspective(440px) rotateX(62deg)\',\n  transformOrigin: \'top center\',\n  backgroundImage:\n    \'linear-gradient(rgba(13,19,22,0.14) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(13,19,22,0.10) 1px, transparent 1px)\',\n  backgroundSize: \'46px 46px\',\n  maskImage: \'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)\',\n  WebkitMaskImage: \'linear-gradient(to top, black 30%, transparent 88%)\',\n};\n\n/**\n * The teacher workspace shell: one top rail that never unmounts, one header,\n * one accent. Every room renders into the `<Outlet/>`; nothing here remounts\n * when the room changes, so room-to-room navigation is real browser history.\n *\n * The rail sits in the header rather than down the side: the shell is entered\n * from the Classroom\'s own side rail, and a second vertical rail immediately\n * inside the first read as two competing navigations. Rooms across the top,\n * each room\'s own tabs (Library\'s bootcamps/drills/assignments/activities) in\n * the workspace below — one level of nav per axis.\n *\n * The AI panel (spec D6) is a Phase 3 build. Its slot and toggle exist now so\n * the layout is settled before the panel lands in it.\n */\nexport default function TeacherWorkspaceShell() {\n  return (\n    <OperatorChatProvider>\n      <WorkspaceLayout />\n    </OperatorChatProvider>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction WorkspaceLayout() {\n  const location = useLocation();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [aiPanelOpen, setAiPanelOpen] = useState(false);\n\n  return (\n    // Fills the classroom stage it is mounted into rather than claiming the\n    // viewport: the classroom rail sits beside it and owns the page height.\n    <div className="relative flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#c3cace] text-[#11181c]">\n      <div aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[56%] overflow-hidden">\n        <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 h-[220%] w-[320%] -translate-x-1/2" style={FLOOR_GRID_STYLE} />\n      </div>\n\n      <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">\n        <header className="shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 sm:px-6">\n          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-3.5">\n            <h1 className="truncate font-display text-[22px] leading-none tracking-tight">\n              Teacher Workspace\n            </h1>\n\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n            <button\n              type="button"\n              onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/whiteboard\')}\n              className={cn(\n                \'inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 text-[12px] font-medium transition\',\n                location.pathname.startsWith(\'/teacher/whiteboard\')\n                  ? \'border-black bg-black text-white\'\n                  : \'border-black/15 text-black/60 hover:border-black/40 hover:text-black\',\n              )}\n            >\n              <PencilRuler className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\n              Whiteboard\n            </button>\n\n            <button\n              type="button"\n              onClick={() => setAiPanelOpen((open) => !open)}\n              aria-expanded={aiPanelOpen}\n              aria-controls="teacher-ai-panel"\n              className={cn(\n                \'inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 text-[12px] font-medium transition\',\n                aiPanelOpen\n                  ? \'border-teacher-accent bg-teacher-accent text-teacher-accent-foreground hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover\'\n                  : \'border-teacher-accent/40 text-teacher-accent-strong hover:bg-teacher-accent/10\',\n              )}\n            >\n              {aiPanelOpen ? (\n                <PanelRightClose className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\n              ) : (\n                <PanelRightOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.6} />\n              )}\n              Assistant\n            </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* The room rail. Hidden below md, where the bottom bar takes over. */}\n          <nav\n            className="hidden items-stretch gap-1 overflow-x-auto md:flex"\n            aria-label="Teacher workspace"\n          >\n            {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (\n              <NavLink\n                key={to}\n                to={to}\n                end={end}\n                className={({ isActive }) =>\n                  cn(\n                    // Deliberately heavier than a room\'s own tab row below it,\n                    // so two stacked horizontal strips can\'t read as the same\n                    // level of navigation.\n                    \'inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b-[3px] px-3.5 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition\',\n                    isActive\n                      ? \'border-teacher-accent font-semibold text-black\'\n                      : \'border-transparent text-black/50 hover:text-black\',\n                  )\n                }\n              >\n                <Icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.5} />\n                <span className="truncate">{label}</span>\n              </NavLink>\n            ))}\n          </nav>\n        </header>\n\n        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">\n          <main className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto">\n            <Outlet />\n          </main>\n\n          {aiPanelOpen ? (\n            <aside\n              id="teacher-ai-panel"\n              className="hidden w-[320px] shrink-0 border-l border-black/10 bg-white/40 md:flex md:flex-col"\n              aria-label="Assistant"\n            >\n              <div className="px-5 pb-3 pt-5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40">\n                Assistant\n              </div>\n              <p className="px-5 text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-black/55">\n                The assistant moves in here, with whatever room you are in as its context. Nothing to use yet.\n              </p>\n            </aside>\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Mobile: the sidebar becomes a bottom bar. Admin has to work on a phone. */}\n        <nav\n          className="flex shrink-0 items-stretch overflow-x-auto border-t border-black/10 bg-[#c3cace] pb-[max(0.25rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] md:hidden"\n          aria-label="Teacher workspace"\n        >\n          {ROOMS.map(({ to, label, Icon, end }) => (\n            <NavLink\n              key={to}\n              to={to}\n              end={end}\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\n                cn(\n                  \'flex min-w-[4.5rem] shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-0.5 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors\',\n                  isActive ? \'text-black\' : \'text-black/45\',\n                )\n              }\n            >\n              <Icon className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={1.5} />\n              <span className="max-w-full truncate px-1">{label}</span>\n            </NavLink>\n          ))}\n        </nav>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\r\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\n===== 4 =====\nimport { useCallback, useState } from 'react';\nimport { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport { toast } from 'sonner';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { isPrimaryAdmin } from '@/lib/adminUsers';\n\n/**\n * Access + role mutations for one student, shared by the dossier's Overview tab.\n *\n * Two defects in the DeskPeopleSurface original are fixed here:\n *\n * 1. It trusted the optimistic write and never refetched, so the row drifted\n *    from the server whenever the Edge Function normalized a value (or a\n *    second admin changed it). We refetch the roster on success and let the\n *    server's answer win.\n * 2. Its rollback restored the WHOLE user object captured before the call, so\n *    a failed role change also reverted any unrelated field that had succeeded\n *    in between. Overrides here are per-field: a failure clears only the key\n *    it touched.\n */\nexport function useStudentAccess(studentId: string | null) {\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\n  // Optimistic overlay. Only holds keys with a write in flight; the roster\n  // query stays the source of truth for everything else.\n  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});\n\n  const clearField = useCallback((field: string) => {\n    setPending((prev) => {\n      if (!(field in prev)) return prev;\n      const next = { ...prev };\n      delete next[field];\n      return next;\n    });\n    setBusy((prev) => {\n      if (!(field in prev)) return prev;\n      const next = { ...prev };\n      delete next[field];\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const refetchRoster = useCallback(async () => {\n    await Promise.all([\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hub-managed-users'] }),\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['teacher-student-profile', studentId] }),\n    ]);\n  }, [qc, studentId]);\n\n  const toggleAccess = useCallback(\n    async (field: string, value: boolean) => {\n      if (!studentId) return;\n      setPending((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));\n      setBusy((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: true }));\n      try {\n        const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {\n          method: 'POST',\n          body: { class_id: studentId, field, value },\n        });\n        if (res.error) throw res.error;\n        // Server is authority: drop the override and pull the real row.\n        await refetchRoster();\n        clearField(field);\n      } catch {\n        // Per-field rollback: this key only, nothing else the user changed.\n        clearField(field);\n        toast.error('Failed to update permission');\n      }\n    },\n    [clearField, refetchRoster, studentId],\n  );\n\n  const setRole = useCallback(\n    async (role: 'admin' | 'user', email: string | null) => {\n      if (!studentId) return;\n      if (role === 'user' && isPrimaryAdmin(email)) {\n        toast.error('The primary admin account cannot be demoted.');\n        return;\n      }\n      setBusy((prev) => ({ ...prev, role: true }));\n      try {\n        const res = await supabase.functions.invoke('admin-manage-users', {\n          method: 'POST',\n          body: { action: 'set_role', user_id: studentId, role },\n        });\n        if (res.error) throw res.error;\n        await refetchRoster();\n        clearField('role');\n        toast.success(role === 'admin' ? 'Promoted to admin' : 'Demoted to user');\n      } catch {\n        clearField('role');\n        toast.error('Role change failed');\n      }\n    },\n    [clearField, refetchRoster, studentId],\n  );\n\n  /** Read a flag through the optimistic overlay. */\n  const readFlag = useCallback(\n    (source: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined, field: string): boolean => {\n      if (field in pending) return pending[field];\n      return Boolean(source?.[field]);\n    },\n    [pending],\n  );\n\n  return { toggleAccess, setRole, readFlag, isBusy: (field: string) => Boolean(busy[field]) };\n}\n\r\n"}]

## assistant

I’m implementing the first liveable slice now: a durable activation request record that is fed by both intake completion and payment, plus an admin queue with approve and cancel actions. That gives LSAT U one reliable answer to “where is this student in the process?” before adding the later CRM view.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 7.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\r\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "../_shared/sendAppEmail.ts";\r\nimport {\r\n  buildOnboardingMarkdown,\r\n  type OnboardingProfile,\r\n} from "../_shared/onboardingMarkdown.ts";\r\n\r\n// Emails Joshua one Markdown summary when a student finishes the intake.\r\n//\r\n// Two ways in, one outcome:\r\n//   - the student\'s own browser calls this right after the completing save;\r\n//   - an admin calls it with { action: "sweep" } to catch anyone whose tab\r\n//     closed before that call landed.\r\n// Both stamp profiles.onboarding_notified_at, so nobody is ever mailed twice.\r\n\r\nconst corsHeaders = {\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",\r\n  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst PROFILE_COLUMNS =\r\n  "class_id, display_name, onboarding, onboarding_completed_at, onboarding_notified_at";\r\n\r\nfunction jsonRes(data: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction adminClient() {\r\n  return createClient(\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!,\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function callerId(req: Request): Promise<string> {\r\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");\r\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")) throw new Error("Missing auth");\r\n  const anon = createClient(\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL")!,\r\n    Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")!,\r\n  );\r\n  const { data, error } = await anon.auth.getClaims(authHeader.slice("Bearer ".length));\r\n  if (error || !data?.claims?.sub) throw new Error("Unauthorized");\r\n  return data.claims.sub as string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Sends for one profile and stamps it. Returns false when nothing was owed. */\r\nasync function notify(\r\n  admin: ReturnType<typeof adminClient>,\r\n  row: Record<string, unknown>,\r\n): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (!row.onboarding_completed_at) return false;\r\n  if (row.onboarding_notified_at) return false;\r\n\r\n  const classId = row.class_id as string;\r\n  const { data: authUser } = await admin.auth.admin.getUserById(classId);\r\n  const profile: OnboardingProfile = {\r\n    class_id: classId,\r\n    display_name: (row.display_name as string) ?? null,\r\n    email: authUser?.user?.email ?? null,\r\n    onboarding: (row.onboarding as Record<string, unknown>) ?? null,\r\n    onboarding_completed_at: row.onboarding_completed_at as string,\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const markdown = buildOnboardingMarkdown(profile);\r\n  await sendAppEmail("onboarding-intake", "", {\r\n    // Same key for the same student forever, so a retry cannot double-send.\r\n    idempotencyKey: `onboarding-intake:${classId}`,\r\n    replyTo: profile.email ?? undefined,\r\n    templateData: {\r\n      studentName: profile.display_name || profile.email || "Unknown student",\r\n      studentEmail: profile.email ?? "",\r\n      markdown,\r\n    },\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // Stamped only after a send that did not throw. A failed send leaves the\r\n  // profile owed, and the next sweep retries it.\r\n  const { error } = await admin\r\n    .from("profiles")\r\n    .update({ onboarding_notified_at: new Date().toISOString() })\r\n    .eq("class_id", classId);\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {\r\n    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const admin = adminClient();\r\n    const body = req.method === "POST" ? await req.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};\r\n\r\n    if (body?.action === "sweep") {\r\n      const uid = await callerId(req);\r\n      const { data: roleRow } = await admin\r\n        .from("user_roles")\r\n        .select("role")\r\n        .eq("user_id", uid)\r\n        .eq("role", "admin")\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (!roleRow) return jsonRes({ error: "Forbidden" }, 403);\r\n\r\n      const { data: owed, error } = await admin\r\n        .from("profiles")\r\n        .select(PROFILE_COLUMNS)\r\n        .not("onboarding_completed_at", "is", null)\r\n        .is("onboarding_notified_at", null);\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      let sent = 0;\r\n      const failed: string[] = [];\r\n      for (const row of owed ?? []) {\r\n        // One student\'s bad address must not strand the rest of the queue.\r\n        try {\r\n          if (await notify(admin, row as Record<string, unknown>)) sent += 1;\r\n        } catch (err) {\r\n          console.error("sweep: send failed", { class_id: row.class_id, err });\r\n          failed.push(row.class_id as string);\r\n        }\r\n'}]

This is the only place a session bundle can\r\n * be synthesized and reviewed — do not drop it when absorbing surfaces.\r\n */\r\nexport default function SessionBundleList({ studentId }: { studentId: string }) {\r\n  const { data: sessions = [] } = useStudentSessions(studentId);\r\n  const { data: drafts = [] } = useAITADrafts({ studentId });\r\n  const { data: context = [] } = useStudentContext(studentId);\r\n\r\n  if (sessions.length === 0) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div className="border border-dashed border-black/12 px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-black/45">\r\n        No sessions logged yet. Ingested Gemini Notes appear here once matched to this student.\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <ul className="space-y-3">\r\n      {sessions.map((s) => (\r\n        <SessionWithBundle\r\n          key={s.id}\r\n          session={s}\r\n          studentClassId={studentId}\r\n          drafts={drafts}\r\n          transcript={context.find((c) => c.id === s.transcript_id) ?? null}\r\n        />\r\n      ))}\r\n    </ul>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction SessionWithBundle({\r\n  session,\r\n  studentClassId,\r\n  drafts,\r\n  transcript,\r\n}: {\r\n  session: {\r\n    id: string;\r\n    scheduled_at: string;\r\n    status: string;\r\n    duration_minutes: number | null;\r\n    source: string | null;\r\n    transcript_id: string | null;\r\n  };\r\n  studentClassId: string;\r\n  drafts: AITADraft[];\r\n  transcript: { content_text: string | null; file_path: string | null } | null;\r\n}) {\r\n  const synth = useSynthesizeBundle();\r\n  const [showTranscript, setShowTranscript] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const bundleDraft = useMemo(\r\n    () =>\r\n      drafts.find(\r\n        (d) =>\r\n          d.draft_type === \'classroom_update_bundle\' &&\r\n          isSessionBundle(d.draft_json) &&\r\n          (d.draft_json as SessionBundle).source?.session_id === session.id,\r\n      ) ?? null,\r\n    [drafts, session.id],\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  async function build() {\r\n    try {\r\n      await synth.mutateAsync({ sessionId: session.id, studentClassId, regenerate: false });\r\n      toast.success(\'Bundle synthesized. Review the parts below.\');\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : \'Synthesis failed\');\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <li className="space-y-3 border border-black/12 bg-white/30 p-4">\r\n      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">\r\n        <div className="min-w-0">\r\n          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#11181c]">\r\n            {format(new Date(session.scheduled_at), \'MMM d, yyyy · h:mm a\')}\r\n          </p>\r\n          <p className="text-xs text-black/45">\r\n            {session.status}\r\n            {session.duration_minutes ? ` · ${session.duration_minutes} min` : \'\'}\r\n            {session.source ? ` · ${session.source.replace(\'_\', \' \')}` : \'\'}\r\n          </p>\r\n        </div>\r\n        {!bundleDraft && session.transcript_id && (\r\n          <button\r\n            onClick={build}\r\n            disabled={synth.isPending}\r\n            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover disabled:opacity-50"\r\n          >\r\n            {synth.isPending ? (\r\n              <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />\r\n            ) : (\r\n              <Wand2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />\r\n            )}\r\n            Build bundle\r\n          </button>\r\n        )}\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {transcript && (\r\n        <div>\r\n          <button\r\n            onClick={() => setShowTranscript((s) => !s)}\r\n            className="text-xs text-black/55 underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-black/80"\r\n          >\r\n            {showTranscript ? \'Hide transcript\' : \'View transcript (admin only)\'}\r\n          </button>\r\n          {showTranscript && (\r\n            <pre className="mt-2 max-h-72 overflow-auto border border-black/12 bg-transparent p-3 text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap text-black/60">\r\n              {transcript.content_text\r\n                ? transcript.content_text\r\n                : transcript.file_path\r\n                  ? \'Transcript is stored in the bucket (too large to inline). Open it from storage.\'\r\n                  : \'No transcript content.\'}\r\n            </pre>\r\n          )}\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n      {bundleDraft && (\r\n        <div className="border-t border-black/12 pt-3">\r\n          <SessionBundleReview draft={bundleDraft} />\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n    </li>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n'}]

I’ll verify whether the existing assignment gates can support that without broadening access.

student_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,\n6:  student_email text not null,\n7:  student_name text,\n8:  product_type text not null check (product_type in (\'package\', \'single_session\')),\n9:  intake_completed_at timestamptz,\n10:  intake_snapshot jsonb,\n11:  payment_confirmed_at timestamptz,\n12:  payment_reference text,\n13:  payment_event_id text unique,\n14:  status text not null default \'pending\' check (status in (\'pending\', \'approved\', \'cancelled\')),\n15:  approved_at timestamptz,\n16:  approved_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,\n17:  cancelled_at timestamptz,\n18:  cancelled_by uuid references auth.users(id) on delete set null,\n19:  notified_at timestamptz,\n20:  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),\n21:  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()\n22:);\n24:create unique index if not exists student_activation_requests_pending_email\n25:  on public.student_activation_requests ## assistant

The review caught one real issue and I fixed it: activation notifications use a constrained notification type, so the migration now explicitly permits `activation_request`. I also made unmatched package payments enter the same activation ledger instead of disappearing into a separate pending path.

The workflow is structurally sound, but I am not going to call it fully working yet. The current recap scanner still stops at a human matching queue, and single-session assignment-only permissions need a finer-grained entitlement than the platform’s current global drill flag.

## assistant

I activated the first implementation slice in an isolated branch:

[LSAT U lifecycle branch](C:\Users\Joshu\dev\lsatu-lifecycle)

Implemented:

- Intake and payment join into one activation request.
- Admin email and LSAT U notification when both are complete.
- Admin queue with Activate and Cancel actions.
- Package approval grants full access.
- Single-session approval grants classroom, chat, scheduling, and future assignment-scoped access.
- Payment records are preserved even if the student account is not yet linked.
- Recap drafts now create an admin notification when generated.
- Existing pre-consultation automation remains active. It found no consultations requiring scheduling in the next five days.

The simplest reliable process is one lifecycle ledger:

`intake → payment → activation queue → admin approval → access → session transcript → recap draft → review → publish`

The system knows where each student is by reading durable records, not by guessing from emails:

- `student_activation_requests`
- `session_ingestions`
- `ai_ta_drafts`
- payment and booking records

What is not yet genuinely complete:

- The branch is not deployed to LSAT U.
- The transcript scanner still requires human matching before synthesis.
- Single-session assignment-only material access needs a finer permission model than the current global drill flag.
- The post-consultation email still needs a scheduled midday-next-day automation.
- Student-folder synchronization is not yet connected to the app lifecycle.

Validation passed for whitespace and patch integrity. The local dependency install hung, so I could not honestly claim a full TypeScript/build pass.

## My request:
&#x20;So, I want to know how this process works. Like, what's going to activate this workflow? Do I have any way to start with where we're at? Is there a way to know if it's going to work? What do I know when I get my next consultation? How is this going to work? What do I do? Do I tell them to start the process? How does it work? Do I trigger it? Like, how do I get this setup going? How will I know when to start? How do I monitor it?


## assistant

Right now, you should not rely on the new workflow yet. It exists in an isolated branch, but it is not deployed to LSAT U.

Once deployed, the process will work like this:

1. Tell the student: “Complete the LSAT U intake form first. Then choose and pay for either a single session or a package. Once payment is confirmed, you can schedule.”

2. The student completes intake.

3. Stripe confirms payment.

4. Those two events automatically create an activation request. You receive an LSAT U notification and an email.

5. You open the Teacher homepage, review the intake and payment, and press the green Activate button.

6. The student receives the appropriate access:

   - Package: full platform access and session credits.
   - Single session: classroom, scheduling, inbox, and only the assigned work.

7. The student schedules from the prominent scheduling button.

For consultations, the process is separate:

- A check-in is sent 30 minutes before the consultation.
- A follow-up is sent around midday the following day.
- A consultation does not automatically activate a student unless they later complete intake and payment.

You monitor the process from two places:

- Teacher homepage: activation requests waiting for your approval.
- Teacher command/review page: recap drafts waiting for approval.

The triggers are intended to be automatic. You should not need to manually start each student’s onboarding. Your only routine action should be reviewing the activation request and approving it.

For the next consultation, the current pre-consultation script is available, but the full LSAT U workflow is not live yet. I would first deploy the branch, run one controlled test using a test student and Stripe test payment, and verify the complete chain before using it with a real student.
